from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import re

from flask import abort, flash, redirect, render_template, request, url_for
from flask_login import current_user

from app import dao, db
from app.models import (
    BenhNhan, DonViThuoc, NhanVien, QuyDinh, Role, TaiKhoan,
    TEN_DON_VI_THUOC_CHUAN, Thuoc,
)
from app.utils import layDuLieuTaiKhoan, yeuCauVaiTro


@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.ADMIN)
def quanLyTaiKhoan():
    keyword = request.args.get("q", "").strip()[:80]
    role_filter = request.args.get("role", "")
    statement = db.select(TaiKhoan)
    if keyword:
        statement = statement.where(
            TaiKhoan.tenDangNhap.contains(keyword, autoescape=True)
            | TaiKhoan.benhNhan.has(BenhNhan.hoTen.contains(keyword, autoescape=True))
            | TaiKhoan.nhanVien.has(NhanVien.hoTen.contains(keyword, autoescape=True))
        )
    if role_filter:
        try:
            selected_role = Role(role_filter)
        except ValueError:
            flash("Bá»™ lá»c vai trÃ² khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.", "danger")
            return redirect(url_for("quan_tri.admin_accounts"))
        statement = statement.where(TaiKhoan.role == selected_role)
    page = db.paginate(statement.order_by(TaiKhoan.tenDangNhap), per_page=10, max_per_page=10)
    return render_template(
        "admin/accounts.html", page=page, keyword=keyword, role_filter=role_filter
    )


@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.ADMIN)
def quanTriThemTaiKhoan():
    error_message = None
    if request.method == "POST":
        try:
            dao.taoTaiKhoan(_kiemTraBieuMauTaiKhoan())
        except ValueError as error:
            db.session.rollback()
            error_message = str(error)
        else:
            flash("ÄÃ£ táº¡o tÃ i khoáº£n vÃ  há»“ sÆ¡ liÃªn quan.", "success")
            if request.args.get("modal") == "1":
                return "", 204
            return redirect(url_for("quan_tri.admin_accounts"))
    return _hienThiBieuMauTaiKhoan(None, request.form, error_message)


@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.ADMIN)
def quanTriSuaTaiKhoan(account_id: int):
    account = db.get_or_404(TaiKhoan, account_id)
    error_message = None
    if request.method == "POST":
        try:
            dao.capNhatTaiKhoan(current_user.maTK, account_id, _kiemTraBieuMauTaiKhoan())
        except ValueError as error:
            db.session.rollback()
            error_message = str(error)
        else:
            flash("ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t tÃ i khoáº£n. Quyá»n má»›i Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ã¡p dá»¥ng tá»« yÃªu cáº§u tiáº¿p theo.", "success")
            if request.args.get("modal") == "1":
                return "", 204
            return redirect(url_for("quan_tri.admin_accounts"))
        values = request.form
    else:
        patient, employee = account.benhNhan, account.nhanVien
        profile = patient if account.role == Role.PATIENT else employee
        values = {
            "tenDangNhap": account.tenDangNhap,
            "role": account.role.value,
            "hoTen": profile.hoTen if profile else "",
            "soDienThoai": profile.soDienThoai if profile else "",
            "trangThai": "on" if account.trangThai else "",
            "namSinh": patient.namSinh if patient else "",
            "gioiTinh": patient.gioiTinh if patient else "",
            "diaChi": patient.diaChi if patient else "",
            "chuyenKhoa": employee.bacSi.chuyenKhoa if employee and employee.bacSi else "",
        }
    return _hienThiBieuMauTaiKhoan(account, values, error_message)


def _kiemTraBieuMauTaiKhoan() -> dict:
    data = layDuLieuTaiKhoan(request.form)
    if data["password"] != request.form.get("xacNhanMatKhau", ""):
        raise ValueError("Máº­t kháº©u xÃ¡c nháº­n khÃ´ng khá»›p.")
    return data


def _hienThiBieuMauTaiKhoan(account, values, error_message):
    modal = request.args.get("modal") == "1"
    template = "components/account_dialog.html" if modal else "admin/account_form.html"
    return render_template(
        template, account=account, values=values, error_message=error_message, modal=modal
    ), 422 if modal and error_message else 200


@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.ADMIN)
def quanTriDoiTrangThaiTaiKhoan(account_id: int):
    active = request.form.get("active")
    if active not in {"0", "1"}:
        abort(400, description="Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i tÃ i khoáº£n khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.")
    try:
        dao.capNhatTrangThaiTaiKhoan(current_user.maTK, account_id, active == "1")
    except ValueError as error:
        flash(str(error), "danger")
    else:
        flash("ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t tráº¡ng thÃ¡i tÃ i khoáº£n.", "success")
    return redirect(url_for(
        "quan_tri.admin_accounts", q=request.form.get("q", ""), role=request.form.get("filter_role", ""),
        page=request.form.get("page", "1"),
    ))


@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.ADMIN)
def quanLyThuoc():
    keyword = request.args.get("q", "").strip()[:100]
    statement = db.select(Thuoc)
    if keyword:
        statement = statement.where(Thuoc.tenThuoc.contains(keyword, autoescape=True))
    page = db.paginate(statement.order_by(Thuoc.maThuoc), per_page=10, max_per_page=10)
    return render_template("admin/catalog.html", page=page, keyword=keyword, unit_mode=False)


@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.ADMIN)
def quanLyDonViThuoc():
    keyword = request.args.get("q", "").strip()[:50]
    statement = db.select(DonViThuoc).where(
        DonViThuoc.tenDonVi.in_(TEN_DON_VI_THUOC_CHUAN)
    )
    if keyword:
        statement = statement.where(DonViThuoc.tenDonVi.contains(keyword, autoescape=True))
    page = db.paginate(statement.order_by(DonViThuoc.maDonVi), per_page=10, max_per_page=10)
    return render_template("admin/catalog.html", page=page, keyword=keyword, unit_mode=True)


def _chinhSuaThuoc(code: str | None):
    item = db.get_or_404(Thuoc, code) if code else None
    error_message = None
    if request.method == "POST":
        try:
            if code is None:
                dao.themThuoc(current_user.maTK, request.form)
            else:
                dao.suaThuoc(current_user.maTK, request.form, code)
        except ValueError as error:
            db.session.rollback()
            error_message = str(error)
        else:
            flash("ÄÃ£ lÆ°u thuá»‘c.", "success")
            if request.args.get("modal") == "1":
                return "", 204
            return redirect(url_for("quan_tri.admin_medicines"))
    if request.method == "POST":
        values = request.form
    elif isinstance(item, Thuoc):
        values = {
            "maThuoc": item.maThuoc, "tenThuoc": item.tenThuoc, "maDonVi": item.maDonVi,
            "donGia": f"{item.donGia:.2f}", "soLuongTon": item.soLuongTon,
        }
    else:
        values = {}
    units = list(db.session.scalars(
        db.select(DonViThuoc)
        .where(DonViThuoc.tenDonVi.in_(TEN_DON_VI_THUOC_CHUAN))
        .order_by(DonViThuoc.tenDonVi)
    ))
    modal = request.args.get("modal") == "1"
    return render_template(
        "components/catalog_dialog.html" if modal else "admin/catalog_form.html",
        values=values, item=item, unit_mode=False, units=units,
        error_message=error_message, modal=modal,
    ), 422 if modal and error_message else 200


@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.ADMIN)
def quanTriChinhSuaThuoc(code: str | None = None):
    return _chinhSuaThuoc(code)


@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.ADMIN)
def quanTriChinhSuaDonVi(code: str | None = None):
    abort(405, description="ÄÆ¡n vá»‹ thuá»‘c Ä‘Æ°á»£c cá»‘ Ä‘á»‹nh theo quy Ä‘á»‹nh.")

    abort(405, description="ÄÆ¡n vá»‹ thuá»‘c Ä‘Æ°á»£c cá»‘ Ä‘á»‹nh theo quy Ä‘á»‹nh.")


@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.ADMIN)
def quanLyQuyDinh():
    error_message = None
    if request.method == "POST":
        try:
            dao.capNhatQuyDinh(current_user.maTK, request.form.get("fee", ""),
                                   request.form.get("maximum", ""))
        except ValueError as error:
            db.session.rollback()
            error_message = str(error)
        else:
            flash("ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t quy Ä‘á»‹nh. KhÃ´ng thay Ä‘á»•i sá»‘ tiá»n trÃªn hÃ³a Ä‘Æ¡n cÅ©.", "success")
            return redirect(url_for("quan_tri.admin_regulations"))
    rules = {rule.maQD: rule for rule in db.session.scalars(db.select(QuyDinh))}
    ready = {"TIEN_KHAM", "SO_BENH_NHAN_TOI_DA"}.issubset(rules)
    if request.method == "GET" and not ready:
        error_message = "ChÆ°a khá»Ÿi táº¡o quy Ä‘á»‹nh. HÃ£y cháº¡y seed-admin-demo."
    values = request.form if request.method == "POST" else {
        "fee": rules["TIEN_KHAM"].giaTri if ready else "",
        "maximum": rules["SO_BENH_NHAN_TOI_DA"].laySoBenhNhanToiDa() if ready else "",
    }
    return render_template("admin/regulations.html", values=values, error_message=error_message,
                           ready=ready)


@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.ADMIN)
def xemBaoCao():
    current = datetime.now(timezone(timedelta(hours=7)))
    selected_month = request.args.get("month", current.strftime("%Y-%m"))
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])", selected_month):
        abort(400, description="ThÃ¡ng bÃ¡o cÃ¡o khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.")
    year, month = map(int, selected_month.split("-"))
    if not 1900 <= year <= 9998:
        abort(400, description="NÄƒm bÃ¡o cÃ¡o pháº£i tá»« 1900 Ä‘áº¿n 9998.")
    report = dao.thongKeHangThang(year, month)
    chart_data = dict(
        days=[row["day"].strftime("%d/%m") for row in report["revenue"]],
        revenue=[float(row["amount"]) for row in report["revenue"]],
        patients=[row["patients"] for row in report["revenue"]],
        medicines=[row["name"] for row in report["medicines"]],
        quantities=[row["quantity"] for row in report["medicines"]],
        uses=[row["uses"] for row in report["medicines"]],
    )
    return render_template("admin/reports.html", report=report,
                           selected_month=selected_month, chart_data=chart_data)

from flask import jsonify, request

from app import app, dao
from app.models import Role
from app.utils import yeuCauVaiTro


@app.route('/cashier/api/patients', methods=['get'], endpoint="cashier_search_patient")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.CASHIER)
def thuNganTimKiemBenhNhan():
    code = request.args.get('kw', '')
    
    patients = dao.thuNganTimBenhNhan(code)
        
    return jsonify(patients=[{
        "code": p.maBN,
        "name": p.hoTen,
    }for p in patients])
        
@app.route('/cashier/api/receipts', methods=['get'], endpoint="get_unpaid_receipts_list")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.CASHIER)
def layDanhSachHoaDonChuaThanhToan():
    patient = dao.timKiemBenhNhanTheoId(request.args.get('ma_bn', ''))
    if patient is None:
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n."), 404

    receipts = dao.layHoaDonChuaThanhToan(ma_bn=patient.maBN)
    if not receipts:
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng cÃ³ hÃ³a Ä‘Æ¡n nÃ o chÆ°a thanh toÃ¡n."), 404

    result = []
    for hd in receipts:
        result.append({
            "id": hd.maHoaDon,
            "date": hd.phieuKham.ngayKham.strftime('%d/%m/%Y'),
            "medical_fee": hd.tienKham,
            "medicine_fee": hd.tienThuoc,
            "total": hd.tongTien
        })
    return jsonify(receipts=result) 

@app.route('/cashier/api/receipts/pay', methods=['post'], endpoint="pay_receipt_api")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.CASHIER)
def apiThanhToan():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    hd = dao.thanhToan(data.get('id'))

    if hd is None:
        return jsonify(error="HÃ³a Ä‘Æ¡n khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i hoáº·c Ä‘Ã£ thanh toÃ¡n."), 400
    return jsonify(message="Thanh toÃ¡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng.")

import re
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from flask import jsonify, request
from flask_login import current_user
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError

from app import app, dao, db
from app.models import BacSi, Role, Thuoc
from app.utils import yeuCauVaiTro


@app.get("/doctor/api/patients", endpoint="doctor_find_patient")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.DOCTOR)
def layThongTinBenhNhan():
    code = request.args.get("code", "").strip()
    if not code:
        return jsonify(error="Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p mÃ£ bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n."), 400
    patient = dao.layThongTinBenhNhan(code)
    if patient is None:
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n vá»›i mÃ£ nÃ y."), 404
    return jsonify(patient={
        "code": patient.maHienThi,
        "name": patient.hoTen,
        "gender": patient.gioiTinh,
        "birth_year": patient.namSinh,
        "phone": patient.soDienThoai,
        "address": patient.diaChi,
    })


@app.get("/doctor/api/patients/<code>/history", endpoint="doctor_patient_history")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.DOCTOR)
def layDanhSachSachLichSu(code: str):
    patient = dao.layThongTinBenhNhan(code)
    if patient is None:
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n vá»›i mÃ£ nÃ y."), 404
    raw_page = request.args.get("page", "1")
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{1,6}", raw_page) or int(raw_page) < 1:
        return jsonify(error="Sá»‘ trang khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡."), 400
    page = int(raw_page)
    page_size = 5
    history, total = dao.layDanhSachSachLichSu(
        patient.maBN,
        page=page,
        page_size=page_size,
    )
    return jsonify(exams=[{
        "id": exam.maPhieuKham,
        "date": exam.ngayKham.strftime("%d/%m/%Y"),
        "symptoms": exam.trieuChung,
        "diagnosis": exam.duDoanLoaiBenh or "",
        "doctor": exam.bacSi.nhanVien.hoTen,
        "prescription_count": len(exam.chiTietDonThuocs),
        "prescriptions": [{
            "code": detail.maThuoc,
            "name": detail.thuoc.tenThuoc,
            "unit": detail.thuoc.donVi.tenDonVi,
            "quantity": detail.soLuong,
            "instructions": detail.cachDung,
        } for detail in exam.chiTietDonThuocs],
        "status": exam.trangThai,
        "can_resume": (
            exam.trangThai == "NHAP"
            and current_user.nhanVien is not None
            and exam.maNV == current_user.nhanVien.maNV
        ),
    } for exam in history], page=page, page_size=page_size, total=total,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size)


@app.get("/doctor/api/exams/<exam_id>", endpoint="doctor_get_draft")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.DOCTOR)
def bacSiLayBanNhap(exam_id: str):
    doctor_id = current_user.nhanVien.maNV if current_user.nhanVien else None
    draft = dao.timBanNhapCuaBacSi(exam_id, doctor_id) if doctor_id is not None else None
    if draft is None:
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y báº£n nhÃ¡p cá»§a bÃ¡c sÄ© nÃ y."), 404
    return jsonify(exam={
        "id": draft.maPhieuKham,
        "patient_code": draft.benhNhan.maHienThi,
        "symptoms": draft.trieuChung,
        "diagnosis": draft.duDoanLoaiBenh or "",
        "prescriptions": [{
            "medicine_code": detail.maThuoc,
            "name": detail.thuoc.tenThuoc,
            "unit": detail.thuoc.donVi.tenDonVi,
            "stock": detail.thuoc.soLuongTon,
            "quantity": detail.soLuong,
            "instructions": detail.cachDung,
        } for detail in draft.chiTietDonThuocs],
    })


@app.get("/doctor/api/drafts", endpoint="doctor_list_drafts")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.DOCTOR)
def bacSiXemDanhSachBanNhap():
    doctor_id = current_user.nhanVien.maNV if current_user.nhanVien else None
    if doctor_id is None:
        return jsonify(error="TÃ i khoáº£n bÃ¡c sÄ© chÆ°a cÃ³ há»“ sÆ¡ chuyÃªn mÃ´n."), 409
    drafts = dao.layBanNhapCuaBacSi(doctor_id)
    return jsonify(drafts=[{
        "id": draft.maPhieuKham,
        "patient_code": draft.benhNhan.maHienThi,
        "patient_name": draft.benhNhan.hoTen,
        "date": draft.ngayKham.strftime("%d/%m/%Y"),
        "symptoms": draft.trieuChung,
        "diagnosis": draft.duDoanLoaiBenh or "",
        "prescription_count": len(draft.chiTietDonThuocs),
    } for draft in drafts])


@app.delete("/doctor/api/drafts/<exam_id>", endpoint="doctor_delete_draft")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.DOCTOR)
def bacSiXoaBanNhap(exam_id: str):
    doctor_id = current_user.nhanVien.maNV if current_user.nhanVien else None
    if doctor_id is None:
        return jsonify(error="TÃ i khoáº£n bÃ¡c sÄ© chÆ°a cÃ³ há»“ sÆ¡ chuyÃªn mÃ´n."), 409
    if not dao.xoaBanNhapCuaBacSi(exam_id, doctor_id):
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y báº£n nhÃ¡p cá»§a bÃ¡c sÄ© nÃ y."), 404
    return jsonify(message="ÄÃ£ xÃ³a báº£n nhÃ¡p.")


@app.get("/doctor/api/medicines", endpoint="doctor_search_medicines")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.DOCTOR)
def layDanhSachThuoc():
    keyword = request.args.get("q", "").strip()
    raw_page = request.args.get("page", "1")
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{1,6}", raw_page) or int(raw_page) < 1:
        return jsonify(error="Sá»‘ trang khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡."), 400
    page = int(raw_page)
    medicines, total = dao.layThongTinThuoc(keyword, page=page)
    return jsonify(medicines=[{
        "code": medicine.maThuoc,
        "name": medicine.tenThuoc,
        "unit": medicine.donVi.tenDonVi,
        "stock": medicine.soLuongTon,
        "price": medicine.donGia,
    } for medicine in medicines], page=page, page_size=10, total=total,
        pages=(total + 9) // 10)


@app.post("/doctor/api/exams", endpoint="doctor_create_exam")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.DOCTOR)
def lapPhieuKham():
    payload = request.get_json(silent=True)
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        return jsonify(error="Dá»¯ liá»‡u phiáº¿u khÃ¡m khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡."), 400

    patient = dao.layThongTinBenhNhan(str(payload.get("patient_code", "")))
    if patient is None:
        return jsonify(error="Vui lÃ²ng tÃ¬m bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n há»£p lá»‡ trÆ°á»›c khi láº­p phiáº¿u."), 400
    symptoms = payload.get("symptoms")
    if not isinstance(symptoms, str) or len(symptoms.strip()) > 2000:
        return jsonify(error="Triá»‡u chá»©ng tá»‘i Ä‘a 2.000 kÃ½ tá»±."), 400
    diagnosis = payload.get("diagnosis", "")
    if not isinstance(diagnosis, str) or len(diagnosis.strip()) > 2000:
        return jsonify(error="Dá»± Ä‘oÃ¡n loáº¡i bá»‡nh tá»‘i Ä‘a 2.000 kÃ½ tá»±."), 400
    status = payload.get("status", "DA_LAP")
    if not isinstance(status, str) or status not in {"NHAP", "DA_LAP"}:
        return jsonify(error="Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i phiáº¿u khÃ¡m khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡."), 400
    if status == "DA_LAP" and not symptoms.strip():
        return jsonify(error="Triá»‡u chá»©ng báº¯t buá»™c khi láº­p phiáº¿u khÃ¡m."), 400
    if status == "DA_LAP" and not diagnosis.strip():
        return jsonify(error="Dá»± Ä‘oÃ¡n loáº¡i bá»‡nh báº¯t buá»™c khi láº­p phiáº¿u khÃ¡m."), 400
    rows = payload.get("prescriptions", [])
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or len(rows) > 30:
        return jsonify(error="Danh sÃ¡ch thuá»‘c khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡."), 400

    doctor = db.session.get(BacSi, current_user.nhanVien.maNV) if current_user.nhanVien else None
    if doctor is None:
        return jsonify(error="TÃ i khoáº£n bÃ¡c sÄ© chÆ°a cÃ³ há»“ sÆ¡ chuyÃªn mÃ´n."), 409

    draft = None
    draft_id = payload.get("draft_id")
    if draft_id is not None:
        if not isinstance(draft_id, str):
            return jsonify(error="MÃ£ báº£n nhÃ¡p khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡."), 400
        draft = dao.timBanNhapCuaBacSi(draft_id, doctor.maNV)
        if draft is None:
            return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y báº£n nhÃ¡p cá»§a bÃ¡c sÄ© nÃ y."), 404
        if draft.maBN != patient.maBN:
            return jsonify(error="Báº£n nhÃ¡p Ä‘Ã£ thuá»™c vá» bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n khÃ¡c."), 409

    prescription_data = []
    seen_medicine_codes = set()
    for row in rows:
        if not isinstance(row, dict):
            return jsonify(error="ThÃ´ng tin thuá»‘c trong Ä‘Æ¡n khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡."), 400
        medicine_code = row.get("medicine_code")
        instructions = row.get("instructions")
        quantity = row.get("quantity")
        if (
            not isinstance(medicine_code, str) or medicine_code in seen_medicine_codes
            or not isinstance(instructions, str)
            or (status == "DA_LAP" and not instructions.strip())
            or len(instructions.strip()) > 255
            or not isinstance(quantity, int) or isinstance(quantity, bool)
            or not 1 <= quantity <= 500
        ):
            return jsonify(error="Vui lÃ²ng kiá»ƒm tra mÃ£ thuá»‘c, sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng vÃ  cÃ¡ch dÃ¹ng."), 400
        seen_medicine_codes.add(medicine_code)
        medicine = db.session.get(Thuoc, medicine_code)
        if medicine is None:
            return jsonify(error=f"KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y thuá»‘c {medicine_code}."), 400
        if quantity > medicine.soLuongTon:
            return jsonify(error=f"Sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng thuá»‘c {medicine.tenThuoc} vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ tá»“n kho."), 400
        prescription_data.append({
            "medicine": medicine,
            "quantity": quantity,
            "instructions": instructions.strip(),
        })

    clinic_timezone = timezone(timedelta(hours=7))
    exam_date = datetime.now(clinic_timezone).date()
    try:
        exam, invoice = dao.lapPhieuKham(
            patient, doctor, symptoms.strip(), diagnosis.strip(), prescription_data, exam_date,
            status=status, exam=draft,
        )
    except ValueError as error:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify(error=str(error)), 409
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify(error="ChÆ°a lÆ°u Ä‘Æ°á»£c phiáº¿u khÃ¡m. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i."), 503
    return jsonify(
        exam_id=exam.maPhieuKham,
        status=exam.trangThai,
        message=(
            "ÄÃ£ lÆ°u báº£n nhÃ¡p." if status == "NHAP"
            else "ÄÃ£ láº­p phiáº¿u khÃ¡m vÃ  hÃ³a Ä‘Æ¡n."
        ),
        invoice_id=invoice.maHoaDon if invoice else None,
        invoice_total=invoice.tongTien if invoice else None,
    ), 201

from datetime import date

from flask import jsonify, render_template, request
from flask_login import current_user
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError

from app import app, dao, db
from app.models import Role
from app.utils import yeuCauVaiTro, guiTinNhanSms


@app.get("/nurse/history", endpoint="nurse_exam_history")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.NURSE)
def lichSuDanhSachKham():
    return render_template(
        "nurse/history.html",
        history=dao.layLichSuDanhSachDaChot(),
    )


def _layNgayKham(value: object) -> date:
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise ValueError("NgÃ y khÃ¡m khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.")
    try:
        return date.fromisoformat(value)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError("NgÃ y khÃ¡m khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.") from None


def _chuyenLuotKhamThanhJson(registration) -> dict:
    patient = registration.benhNhan
    return {
        "listId": registration.maDSKham,
        "patientId": registration.maBN,
        "name": patient.hoTen,
        "gender": patient.gioiTinh,
        "birthYear": patient.namSinh,
        "phone": patient.soDienThoai,
        "address": patient.diaChi,
        "status": registration.trangThai,
        "slot": registration.soThuTu,
        "examDate": registration.ngayKham.isoformat(),
    }


@app.get("/nurse/api/lists", endpoint="nurse_exam_lists")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.NURSE)
def layDanhSachKhamYTa():
    try:
        exam_date = _layNgayKham(request.args.get("date"))
        limit = dao.layGioiHanBenhNhanNgay()
        active, waiting = dao.layDanhSachKham(exam_date)
    except ValueError as error:
        return jsonify(error=str(error)), 400
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        db.session.rollback()
        app.logger.exception("Nurse exam list read failed.")
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i danh sÃ¡ch khÃ¡m tá»« cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ dá»¯ liá»‡u."), 500
    confirmed = bool(active) and all(
        entry.trangThai == "DA_XAC_NHAN" for entry in active
    )
    locked = any(entry.trangThai == "DA_XAC_NHAN" for entry in active)
    return jsonify(
        date=exam_date.isoformat(),
        limit=limit,
        active=[_chuyenLuotKhamThanhJson(entry) for entry in active],
        waiting=[_chuyenLuotKhamThanhJson(entry) for entry in waiting],
        confirmed=confirmed,
        locked=locked,
        today=dao.ngayHienTaiPhongKham().isoformat(),
    )


@app.get("/nurse/api/patients", endpoint="nurse_find_patient")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.NURSE)
def yTaTimBenhNhan():
    query = request.args.get("q", "").strip()
    patients = dao.timBenhNhanTheoMa(query) if query else []
    return jsonify(patients=[{
        "code": patient.maHienThi,
        "name": patient.hoTen,
        "gender": patient.gioiTinh,
        "birth_year": patient.namSinh,
        "phone": patient.soDienThoai,
    } for patient in patients])


@app.post("/nurse/api/lists", endpoint="nurse_create_exam")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.NURSE)
def yTaThemLuotKham():
    try:
        payload = request.get_json(silent=True)
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            return jsonify(error="Dá»¯ liá»‡u yÃªu cáº§u khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡."), 400
        patient_code = payload.get("patient_code")
        if not isinstance(patient_code, str) or not patient_code.strip():
            return jsonify(error="Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p mÃ£ bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n."), 400
        patient = dao.timKiemBenhNhanTheoId(patient_code)
        if patient is None:
            return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n vá»›i mÃ£ nÃ y."), 404
        exam_date = _layNgayKham(payload.get("date"))
        nurse = current_user.nhanVien
        registration = dao.themBenhNhan(
            exam_date=exam_date,
            patient=patient,
            nurse_id=nurse.maNV if nurse else None,
        )
    except ValueError as error:
        db.session.rollback()
        status_code = (
            409
            if isinstance(error, (dao.DailyCapacityReached, dao.ConfirmedExamDay))
            else 400
        )
        return jsonify(error=str(error)), status_code
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        db.session.rollback()
        app.logger.exception("Nurse patient registration failed.")
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ lÆ°u lÆ°á»£t khÃ¡m vÃ o cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ dá»¯ liá»‡u."), 500
    return jsonify(patient=_chuyenLuotKhamThanhJson(registration),
                   message="ÄÃ£ lÆ°u bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n vÃ o danh sÃ¡ch khÃ¡m."), 201


@app.delete(
    "/nurse/api/lists/<int:maDSKham>/<int:maBN>",
    endpoint="nurse_cancel_exam",
)
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.NURSE)
def yTaHuyLuotKham(maDSKham: int, maBN: int):
    try:
        dao.huyLuotKham(maDSKham, maBN)
    except LookupError as error:
        return jsonify(error=str(error)), 404
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        db.session.rollback()
        app.logger.exception("Nurse exam cancellation failed.")
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ há»§y lÆ°á»£t khÃ¡m trong cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ dá»¯ liá»‡u."), 500
    return jsonify(message="ÄÃ£ há»§y lÆ°á»£t khÃ¡m.")


@app.post("/nurse/api/lists/confirm", endpoint="nurse_confirm_exam_day")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.NURSE)
def yTaXacNhanDanhSach():
    try:
        payload = request.get_json(silent=True)
        exam_date = _layNgayKham(payload.get("date") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None)
        nurse = current_user.nhanVien
        sms_entries = dao.xacNhanDanhSach(
            exam_date,
            nurse_id=nurse.maNV if nurse else None,
        )
    except ValueError as error:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify(error=str(error)), 409
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        db.session.rollback()
        app.logger.exception("Nurse exam-list confirmation failed.")
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ¡c nháº­n danh sÃ¡ch trong cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ dá»¯ liá»‡u."), 500

    if not sms_entries:
        return jsonify(message="Danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c xÃ¡c nháº­n trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³.")
    sent = failed = 0
    for entry in sms_entries:
        patient = entry.benhNhan
        code = f"{entry.soThuTu or 0:02d}"   # pháº§n biáº¿n, tá»‘i Ä‘a 20 kÃ½ tá»±
        content = f"{code} la ma xac minh dang ky Baotrixemay cua ban"
        #f"Lich kham: {exam_date.strftime('%d/%m/%Y')}, STT: {entry.soThuTu}."
        ok, data = guiTinNhanSms(patient.soDienThoai.lstrip("+"), content)
        if ok:
            sent += 1
        else:
            failed += 1
            app.logger.warning("SMS that bai cho benh nhan %s: %s", patient.maBN, data)

    return jsonify(message=f"Danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c xÃ¡c nháº­n. ÄÃ£ gá»­i {sent} SMS, lá»—i {failed}.")


@app.post("/nurse/api/lists/reopen", endpoint="nurse_reopen_exam_day")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.NURSE)
def yTaMoLaiDanhSach():
    try:
        payload = request.get_json(silent=True)
        exam_date = _layNgayKham(
            payload.get("date") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None
        )
        reopened_count = dao.moLaiDanhSachKham(exam_date)
    except ValueError as error:
        db.session.rollback()
        return jsonify(error=str(error)), 409
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        db.session.rollback()
        app.logger.exception("Nurse exam-list reopen failed.")
        return jsonify(error="KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ má»Ÿ láº¡i danh sÃ¡ch trong cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ dá»¯ liá»‡u."), 500
    return jsonify(
        message=(
            "ÄÃ£ má»Ÿ láº¡i danh sÃ¡ch khÃ¡m Ä‘á»ƒ bá»• sung bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n. "
            f"CÃ³ {reopened_count} bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n cáº§n xÃ¡c nháº­n láº¡i."
        ),
        reopened_count=reopened_count,
    )

import re

from flask import abort, flash, redirect, render_template, request, url_for
from flask_login import current_user, login_required

from app import app, dao, db
from app.models import Role
from app.utils import yeuCauVaiTro


def _hienThiHoSoBenhNhan(patient, form_values=None, status: int = 200):
    return render_template(
        "patient/profile.html",
        patient=patient,
        form_values=form_values or {},
    ), status


@app.route("/patient/profile", methods=["GET", "POST"], endpoint="patient_profile")
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.PATIENT)
def hoSoBenhNhan():
    patient = current_user.benhNhan
    if patient is None:
        abort(400, description="TÃ i khoáº£n bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n chÆ°a cÃ³ há»“ sÆ¡.")
    if request.method == "POST":
        name = request.form.get("hoTen", "").strip()
        phone = request.form.get("soDienThoai", "").strip()
        address = request.form.get("diaChi", "").strip()
        error = None
        if not name or len(name) > 100:
            error = "Há» tÃªn báº¯t buá»™c vÃ  tá»‘i Ä‘a 100 kÃ½ tá»±."
        elif not re.fullmatch(r"\+?[0-9]{9,15}", phone):
            error = "Sá»‘ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i pháº£i cÃ³ 9â€“15 chá»¯ sá»‘, cÃ³ thá»ƒ báº¯t Ä‘áº§u báº±ng +."
        elif not address or len(address) > 255:
            error = "Äá»‹a chá»‰ báº¯t buá»™c vÃ  tá»‘i Ä‘a 255 kÃ½ tá»±."

        if error:
            flash(error, "danger")
            return _hienThiHoSoBenhNhan(patient, request.form, status=400)
        try:
            dao.capNhatHoSoBenhNhan(
                patient.maBN,
                name=name,
                phone=phone,
                address=address,
            )
        except LookupError:
            abort(404, description="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y há»“ sÆ¡ bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n.")
        except ValueError as error:
            db.session.rollback()
            flash(str(error), "danger")
            return _hienThiHoSoBenhNhan(patient, request.form, status=409)

        flash("ThÃ´ng tin bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº­p nháº­t.", "success")
        return redirect(url_for("patient_profile"))
    return _hienThiHoSoBenhNhan(patient)


def hienThiTrangBenhNhan(status: int = 200):
    patient = current_user.benhNhan
    if patient is None:
        abort(400, description="TÃ i khoáº£n bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n chÆ°a cÃ³ há»“ sÆ¡.")
    return render_template(
        "patient/dashboard.html",
        patient=patient,
        registrations=dao.layDangKyKhamCuaBenhNhan(patient.maBN),
    ), status


@app.post("/patient/exam-registrations", endpoint="patient_register_exam")
@login_required
@yeuCauVaiTro(Role.PATIENT)
def benhNhanDangKyKham():
    patient = current_user.benhNhan
    if patient is None:
        abort(400, description="TÃ i khoáº£n bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n chÆ°a cÃ³ há»“ sÆ¡.")

    try:
        registration = dao.dangKyKham(patient)
    except ValueError as error:
        db.session.rollback()
        flash(str(error), "danger")
        return hienThiTrangBenhNhan(status=409)
    if registration.ngayKham > dao.ngayHienTaiPhongKham():
        flash(
            "Danh sÃ¡ch hÃ´m nay Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘áº§y hoáº·c Ä‘Ã£ chá»‘t. Há»“ sÆ¡ Ä‘Æ°á»£c chuyá»ƒn sang ngÃ y gáº§n nháº¥t cÃ²n chá»—; ngÃ y khÃ¡m sáº½ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ sau khi y tÃ¡ xÃ¡c nháº­n.",
            "success",
        )
    else:
        flash("ÄÄƒng kÃ½ khÃ¡m thÃ nh cÃ´ng. Há»“ sÆ¡ Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c thÃªm vÃ o danh sÃ¡ch khÃ¡m.", "success")
    return redirect(url_for("dashboard"))

=== FILE: C:\Tloc place\CNPM\code\app\static\js\admin\accounts.js ===
function capNhatTruongTheoVaiTro(root = document) {
    const selector = root.querySelector("[data-role-select]");
    const selected = selector ? selector.value : "PATIENT";
    const phone = root.querySelector("#phone");
    if (phone) phone.required = selected === "PATIENT";
    root.querySelectorAll("[data-role-fields]").forEach(section => {
        const enabled = section.dataset.roleFields === selected;
        section.hidden = !enabled;
        section.querySelectorAll("input, select").forEach(field => {
            field.disabled = !enabled;
            field.required = enabled;
        });
    });
}

document.addEventListener("change", event => {
    if (event.target.matches("[data-role-select]")) {
        capNhatTruongTheoVaiTro(event.target.closest("form"));
    }
});
capNhatTruongTheoVaiTro();
const modalElement = document.querySelector("#account-modal");
if (modalElement) {
    const content = modalElement.querySelector(".modal-content");
    const errorBox = document.querySelector("#modal-error");
    let loading = false;
    function hienThiLoi(message) {
        errorBox.textContent = message;
        errorBox.hidden = false;
        errorBox.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
    }
    document.addEventListener("click", async event => {
        const link = event.target.closest("[data-account-modal]");
        if (!link || !window.bootstrap || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.shiftKey) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        if (loading) return;
        loading = true;
        errorBox.hidden = true;
        const url = new URL(link.href);
        url.searchParams.set("modal", "1");
        try {
            const response = await fetch(url);
            if (response.redirected) {
                window.location.assign(response.url);
                return;
            }
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error("KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c biá»ƒu máº«u. Vui lÃ²ng táº£i láº¡i trang hoáº·c thá»­ láº¡i.");
            content.innerHTML = await response.text();
            capNhatTruongTheoVaiTro(content);
            bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(modalElement).show();
        } catch (error) {
            hienThiLoi(error.message);
        } finally {
            loading = false;
        }
    });
    document.addEventListener("submit", async event => {
        const form = event.target;
        if (!form.matches("[data-modal-form]")) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        if (form.dataset.saving) return;
        form.dataset.saving = "true";
        const button = form.querySelector('button:not([type="button"])');
        button.disabled = true;
        errorBox.hidden = true;
        try {
            const response = await fetch(form.action, { method: "POST", body: new FormData(form) });
            if (response.redirected) {
                window.location.assign(response.url);
            } else if (response.status === 204) {
                window.location.reload();
            } else if (response.status === 422) {
                content.innerHTML = await response.text();
                capNhatTruongTheoVaiTro(content);
                content.querySelector("[role='alert']")?.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
            } else {
                throw new Error("KhÃ´ng lÆ°u Ä‘Æ°á»£c tÃ i khoáº£n. Vui lÃ²ng kiá»ƒm tra káº¿t quáº£ trÆ°á»›c khi thá»­ láº¡i.");
            }
        } catch (error) {
            hienThiLoi(error.message);
            bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalElement)?.hide();
        } finally {
            button.disabled = false;
            delete form.dataset.saving;
        }
    });
}

(() => {
    const searchForm = document.querySelector("[data-catalog-search]");
    const errorBox = document.querySelector("#catalog-error");

    const content = modalElement.querySelector(".modal-content");

    let loading = false;

        errorBox.textContent = message;
        errorBox.hidden = false;
        errorBox.scrollIntoView({block: "nearest"});
    };
    searchForm.querySelector("input").addEventListener("input", () => {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        const requestId = ++sequence;
        timer = setTimeout(async () => {
            const url = new URL(searchForm.action);
            url.searchParams.set("q", searchForm.querySelector("input").value);
            try {
                const response = await fetch(url);
                if (requestId !== sequence) return;
                if (response.redirected) {
                    window.location.assign(response.url);
                    return;
                }
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error("KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c danh má»¥c. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.");
                const documentResult = new DOMParser().parseFromString(await response.text(), "text/html");
                if (requestId !== sequence) return;
                const results = documentResult.querySelector("[data-catalog-results]");
                if (!results) throw new Error("Dá»¯ liá»‡u danh má»¥c khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡. Vui lÃ²ng táº£i láº¡i trang.");
                document.querySelector("[data-catalog-results]").replaceWith(results);
                history.replaceState(null, "", url);
                errorBox.hidden = true;
            } catch (error) {
                if (requestId === sequence) hienThiLoi(error.message);
            }
        }, 300);
    });
    document.addEventListener("click", async event => {
        const link = event.target.closest("[data-catalog-modal]");
        if (!link || !window.bootstrap || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.shiftKey) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        if (loading) return;
        loading = true;
        errorBox.hidden = true;
        const url = new URL(link.href);
        url.searchParams.set("modal", "1");
        try {
            const response = await fetch(url);
            if (response.redirected) {
                window.location.assign(response.url);
                return;
            }
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error("KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c biá»ƒu máº«u. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.");
            content.innerHTML = await response.text();
            bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(modalElement).show();
        } catch (error) {
            hienThiLoi(error.message);
        } finally {
            loading = false;
        }
    });
    document.addEventListener("submit", async event => {
        const form = event.target;
        if (form.matches("[data-catalog-delete]")) {
            if (!window.confirm("XÃ³a " + form.dataset.code + "? Thao tÃ¡c nÃ y khÃ´ng thá»ƒ hoÃ n tÃ¡c.")) event.preventDefault();
            return;
        }
        if (!form.matches("[data-catalog-form]")) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        if (form.dataset.saving) return;
        form.dataset.saving = "true";
        const button = form.querySelector('button:not([type="button"])');
        button.disabled = true;
        try {
            const response = await fetch(form.action, {method: "POST", body: new FormData(form)});
            if (response.redirected) window.location.assign(response.url);
            else if (response.status === 204) window.location.reload();
            else if (response.status === 422) content.innerHTML = await response.text();

            delete form.dataset.saving;

=== FILE: C:\Tloc place\CNPM\code\app\static\js\cashier.js ===
let receiptPaidState = "DA_THANH_TOAN";
let receiptUnpaidState = "CHUA_THANH_TOAN";

function timKiemBenhNhan() {
    let kw = document.getElementById("kw").value.trim();
    let list = document.getElementById("list");

    if (!kw) {
        lamMoiBieuMauThuNgan();
        return;
    }
    fetch(`/cashier/api/patients?kw=${encodeURIComponent(kw)}`)
        .then((response) => {
            if (!response.ok) {
                return response.json().then((err) => {
                    throw new Error(err.error);
                });
            }
            return response.json();
        })
        .then((data) => {
            let patients = data.patients || [];

            if (patients.length === 0) {
                list.innerHTML =
                    '<li class="list-group-item text-muted">KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n</li>';
                list.classList.remove("d-none");
                return;
            }

            let html = "";

            patients.forEach((p) => {
                html += `
                    <li class="list-group-item list-group-item-action"
                        style="cursor: pointer;"
                        onclick="chonBenhNhan('${p.code}', '${p.name}')">
                        <strong>${p.name}</strong>
                        <small class="text-muted">(${p.code})</small>
                    </li>`;
            });
            list.innerHTML = html;
            list.classList.remove("d-none");
        })
        .catch((err) => {
            list.innerHTML = `<li class="list-group-item text-danger">${err.message}</li>`;
            list.classList.remove("d-none");
        });
}

function chonBenhNhan(patientCode, patientName) {
    document.getElementById("kw").value = patientName;
    let list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    list.classList.add("d-none");

    layDanhSachHoaDonChuaThanhToan(patientCode);
}

let currentReceipt = [];

function layDanhSachHoaDonChuaThanhToan(patientCode) {
    let dropdown = document.getElementById("receipts_list");

    fetch(`/cashier/api/receipts?ma_bn=${encodeURIComponent(patientCode)}`)
        .then((response) => {
            if (!response.ok) {
                return response.json().then((err) => {
                    throw new Error(err.error || "Lá»—i táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u");
                });
            }
            return response.json();
        })
        .then((data) => {
            currentReceipt = data.receipts || [];
            let html = '<option value="">-- Chá»n hÃ³a Ä‘Æ¡n cáº§n thanh toÃ¡n --</option>';

            currentReceipt.forEach((r) => {
                html += `<option value="${r.id}">
                            HÄ #${r.id} - NgÃ y khÃ¡m: ${r.date} - Tá»•ng: ${r.total.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VNÄ
                         </option>`;
            });
            dropdown.innerHTML = html;
            capNhatHoaDon(null);
        })
        .catch((err) => {
            dropdown.innerHTML = `<option value="">${err.message}</option>`;
        });
}

function chonHoaDon() {
    let id = document.getElementById("receipts_list").value;
    let receipt = currentReceipt.find((r) => String(r.id) === id);
    capNhatHoaDon(receipt || null);
}

function capNhatHoaDon(r) {
    document.getElementById("hoTenBN").innerText = r
        ? document.getElementById("kw").value
        : "---";
    document.getElementById("ngayKham").innerText = r ? r.date : "---";
    document.getElementById("tienKham").innerText =
        (r ? r.medical_fee : 0).toLocaleString("vi-VN") + " VNÄ";
    document.getElementById("tienThuoc").innerText =
        (r ? r.medicine_fee : 0).toLocaleString("vi-VN") + " VNÄ";
    document.getElementById("tongTien").innerText =
        (r ? r.total : 0).toLocaleString("vi-VN") + " VNÄ";
    document.getElementById("btnThanhToan").disabled = !r;
}

function lamMoiBieuMauThuNgan() {
    document.getElementById("kw").value = "";

    let list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    list.classList.add("d-none");

    document.getElementById("receipts_list").innerHTML =
        '<option value="" selected disabled hidden> --Danh sÃ¡ch hÃ³a Ä‘Æ¡n chÆ°a thanh toÃ¡n-- </option>';

    currentReceipt = [];
    capNhatHoaDon(null);
}

function thanhToan() {
    let id = document.getElementById("receipts_list").value;
    if (!id) return;

    fetch("/cashier/api/receipts/pay", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "X-CSRF-Token": document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: id }),
    })
        .then((response) =>
            response.json().then((data) => {
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error(data.error || "Lá»—i thanh toÃ¡n");
                }
                return data;
            }),
        )
        .then((data) => {
            alert(data.message);
            lamMoiBieuMauThuNgan();
        })
        .catch((err) => alert(err.message));
}

    async function docJson(response) {
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || "YÃªu cáº§u khÃ´ng thÃ nh cÃ´ng.");
        return data;
    }

    function themO(row, value) {
        const cell = document.createElement("td");
        cell.textContent = value;
        row.append(cell);
        return cell;
    }

    function hienThiPhanTrangLichSu(page, pages) {
        historyPagination.replaceChildren();
        if (pages <= 1) return;

        function themNutTrang(label, target, disabled = false, current = false) {
            const button = document.createElement("button");
            button.type = "button";
            button.className = current
                ? "btn btn-sm btn-primary"
                : "btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary";
            button.textContent = label;
            button.dataset.historyPage = String(target);
            button.disabled = disabled;
            if (current) button.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
            historyPagination.append(button);
        }

        themNutTrang("â€¹ TrÆ°á»›c", page - 1, page === 1);
        const firstPage = Math.max(1, Math.min(page - 2, pages - 4));
        const lastPage = Math.min(pages, firstPage + 4);
        for (let target = firstPage; target <= lastPage; target += 1) {
            themNutTrang(String(target), target, false, target === page);
        }
        themNutTrang("Sau â€º", page + 1, page === pages);
    }

    function hienThiBenhNhan(patient) {
        selectedPatientCode = patient.code;
        patientCard.hidden = false;
        document.querySelector("#patient-code").textContent = patient.code;

    }

    async function layThongTinBenhNhan() {
        const code = patientQuery.value.trim();
        if (!code) {
            selectedPatientCode = "";
            patientCard.hidden = true;
            hienThiThongBaoLichSu(
                "ChÆ°a nháº­p mÃ£ bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n. Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p mÃ£ bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n Ä‘á»ƒ xem danh sÃ¡ch phiáº¿u khÃ¡m."
            );
            patientQuery.focus();
            return;
        }
        try {
            const response = await fetch(
                `/doctor/api/patients?code=${encodeURIComponent(code)}`
            );
            const data = await docJson(response);
            hienThiBenhNhan(data.patient);
            if (document.querySelector("#symptoms").value.trim()
                || document.querySelector("#diagnosis").value.trim()
                || selectedMedicines.size) {
                henTuDongLuuBanNhap();
            }
        } catch (error) {
            selectedPatientCode = "";
            patientCard.hidden = true;
            hienThiThongBaoLichSu(error.message);
        }
    }

    async function layDanhSachSachLichSu(page = 1) {
        if (!selectedPatientCode) {
            await layThongTinBenhNhan();
            if (!selectedPatientCode) return;
        }
        const requestPatientCode = selectedPatientCode;
        const requestId = ++historyRequestId;
        try {
            const url = new URL(
                `/doctor/api/patients/${encodeURIComponent(requestPatientCode)}/history`,
                window.location.origin
            );
            url.searchParams.set("page", String(page));
            const response = await fetch(
                url
            );
            const data = await docJson(response);
            if (requestId !== historyRequestId || requestPatientCode !== selectedPatientCode) {
                return;
            }
            if (!data.exams.length) {
                hienThiThongBaoLichSu("Bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n chÆ°a cÃ³ phiáº¿u khÃ¡m trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³.");
                return;
            }
            hienThiLichSuKham(data);
        } catch (error) {
            if (requestId === historyRequestId) {
                hienThiThongBaoLichSu(error.message);
            }
        }
    }

    async function tiepTucBanNhap(examId) {
        try {
            const response = await fetch(`/doctor/api/exams/${encodeURIComponent(examId)}`);
            const data = await docJson(response);
            const patientResponse = await fetch(
                `/doctor/api/patients?code=${encodeURIComponent(data.exam.patient_code)}`
            );
            const patientData = await docJson(patientResponse);
            hienThiBenhNhan(patientData.patient);
            datMaBanNhapHienTai(data.exam.id);
            document.querySelector("#symptoms").value = data.exam.symptoms;
            document.querySelector("#diagnosis").value = data.exam.diagnosis || "";
            selectedMedicines.clear();
            data.exam.prescriptions.forEach(item => {
                selectedMedicines.set(item.medicine_code, {
                    code: item.medicine_code,
                    name: item.name,
                    unit: item.unit,
                    stock: item.stock,
                    quantity: item.quantity,
                    instructions: item.instructions,
                    editing: false,
                });
            });
            hienThiDonThuoc();
            hienThiTrangThaiBacSi(`Äang tiáº¿p tá»¥c báº£n nhÃ¡p ${examId}.`);
            await layDanhSachSachLichSu();
            await taiDanhSachBanNhap();
            document.querySelector("#exam-details").scrollIntoView({
                block: "start", behavior: "smooth",
            });
        } catch (error) {
            hienThiTrangThaiBacSi(error.message, true);
        }
    }

    async function taiDanhSachBanNhap() {
        try {
            const response = await fetch("/doctor/api/drafts");
            const data = await docJson(response);
            draftsRows.replaceChildren();
            draftsCount.textContent = String(data.drafts.length);
            draftsEmpty.hidden = data.drafts.length > 0;
            draftsTable.hidden = data.drafts.length === 0;
            data.drafts.forEach(draft => {
                const row = document.createElement("tr");
                themO(row, draft.date);
                themO(row, `${draft.patient_name} (${draft.patient_code})`);
                themO(row, draft.symptoms || "ChÆ°a nháº­p");
                themO(row, draft.diagnosis || "ChÆ°a nháº­p");
                themO(row, String(draft.prescription_count));
                const action = document.createElement("td");
                const button = document.createElement("button");
                button.type = "button";
                button.className = "btn btn-outline-primary btn-sm";
                button.dataset.resumeDraft = draft.id;
                button.textContent = "Má»Ÿ báº£n nhÃ¡p";
                const removeButton = document.createElement("button");
                removeButton.type = "button";
                removeButton.className = "btn btn-outline-danger btn-sm ms-2";
                removeButton.dataset.deleteDraft = draft.id;
                removeButton.textContent = "XÃ³a";
                action.append(button, removeButton);
                row.append(action);
                draftsRows.append(row);
            });
        } catch (error) {
            draftsCount.textContent = "!";
            draftsEmpty.textContent = error.message;
            draftsEmpty.hidden = false;
            draftsTable.hidden = true;
        }
    }

    async function khoiPhucBanNhapDangLam() {
        const draftId = sessionStorage.getItem(activeDraftStorageKey);
        if (!draftId) return;
        await tiepTucBanNhap(draftId);
    }

    async function xoaBanNhap(draftId, button) {
        if (!window.confirm("Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c muá»‘n xÃ³a báº£n nhÃ¡p nÃ y? Thao tÃ¡c nÃ y khÃ´ng thá»ƒ hoÃ n tÃ¡c.")) {
            return;
        }
        button.disabled = true;
        try {
            if (draftId === currentDraftId) {
                window.clearTimeout(autosaveTimer);
                await autosaveInFlight;
            }
            const response = await fetch(
                `/doctor/api/drafts/${encodeURIComponent(draftId)}`,
                {
                    method: "DELETE",
                    headers: {
                        "X-CSRF-Token": document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content,
                    },
                }
            );
            const result = await docJson(response);
            if (draftId === currentDraftId) {
                datMaBanNhapHienTai(null);
                draftDirty = false;
                draftRevision += 1;
                document.querySelector("#symptoms").value = "";
                document.querySelector("#diagnosis").value = "";
                selectedMedicines.clear();
                hienThiDonThuoc();
            }
            hienThiTrangThaiBacSi(result.message);
            await taiDanhSachBanNhap();
            if (selectedPatientCode) await layDanhSachSachLichSu();
        } catch (error) {
            hienThiTrangThaiBacSi(error.message, true);
        } finally {
            button.disabled = false;
        }
    }

    function hienThiPhanTrangThuoc(page, pages) {

    async function traCuuThuoc(page = 1) {
        window.clearTimeout(medicineSearchTimer);
        medicineSearchTimer = null;
        const keyword = medicineQuery.value.trim();
        const requestId = ++medicineRequestId;
        medicineResults.replaceChildren();
        medicineSummary.textContent = "Äang táº£i danh sÃ¡ch thuá»‘c...";
        try {
            const url = new URL("/doctor/api/medicines", window.location.origin);
            url.searchParams.set("page", String(page));
            if (keyword) url.searchParams.set("q", keyword);
            const response = await fetch(url);
            const data = await docJson(response);
            if (requestId !== medicineRequestId) return;
            if (!data.medicines.length) {
                const row = document.createElement("tr");
                const cell = themO(
                    row,
                    keyword
                        ? "KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y thuá»‘c cÃ²n tá»“n kho phÃ¹ há»£p."
                        : "ChÆ°a cÃ³ thuá»‘c nÃ o cÃ²n tá»“n kho."
                );
                cell.colSpan = 6;
                cell.className = "doctor-table-empty";
                medicineResults.append(row);
            } else {
                data.medicines.forEach(medicine => {
                    const row = document.createElement("tr");
                    themO(row, medicine.code);
                    themO(row, medicine.name);
                    themO(row, medicine.unit);
                    themO(row, String(medicine.stock));
                    themO(
                        row,
                        `${new Intl.NumberFormat("vi-VN").format(medicine.price)} VNÄ`
                    );
                    const action = document.createElement("td");
                    const button = document.createElement("button");
                    button.type = "button";
                    button.className = "btn btn-outline-primary btn-sm";
                    button.dataset.addMedicine = medicine.code;
                    button.textContent = "ThÃªm";
                    action.append(button);
                    row.append(action);
                    medicineResults.append(row);
                });
            }
            const first = data.total ? (data.page - 1) * data.page_size + 1 : 0;
            const last = Math.min(data.page * data.page_size, data.total);
            medicineSummary.textContent = data.total
                ? `Hiá»ƒn thá»‹ ${first}â€“${last} trÃªn ${data.total} thuá»‘c`
                : "KhÃ´ng cÃ³ thuá»‘c phÃ¹ há»£p.";
            hienThiPhanTrangThuoc(data.page, data.pages);
        } catch (error) {
            if (requestId === medicineRequestId) {
                medicineSummary.textContent = "KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c danh sÃ¡ch thuá»‘c.";
                hienThiTrangThaiBacSi(error.message, true);
            }
        }
    }

    function henTimKiemThuoc() {
        window.clearTimeout(medicineSearchTimer);
        medicineRequestId += 1;
        medicineSearchTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
            medicineSearchTimer = null;
            traCuuThuoc(1);
        }, 250);
    }

    function hienThiDonThuoc() {
        prescriptionItems.replaceChildren();
        if (!selectedMedicines.size) {
            const row = document.createElement("tr");
            const cell = themO(row, "ChÆ°a cÃ³ thuá»‘c trong Ä‘Æ¡n.");
            cell.colSpan = 5;
            cell.className = "doctor-table-empty";
            prescriptionItems.append(row);
            return;
        }
        selectedMedicines.forEach(medicine => {
            const row = document.createElement("tr");
            themO(row, `${medicine.name} (${medicine.code})`);
            themO(row, medicine.unit);

            const quantityCell = document.createElement("td");
            const quantity = document.createElement("input");
            quantity.type = "number";
            quantity.min = "1";
            quantity.max = String(medicine.stock);
            quantity.value = String(medicine.editQuantity ?? medicine.quantity);
            quantity.disabled = !medicine.editing;
            quantity.className = "form-control form-control-sm";
            quantity.setAttribute("aria-label", `Sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng ${medicine.name}`);
            quantity.addEventListener("input", () => {
                medicine.editQuantity = Number(quantity.value);
            });
            quantityCell.append(quantity);
            row.append(quantityCell);

            const instructionCell = document.createElement("td");
            const instructions = document.createElement("input");
            instructions.type = "text";
            instructions.maxLength = 255;
            instructions.value = medicine.editInstructions ?? medicine.instructions;
            instructions.disabled = !medicine.editing;
            instructions.placeholder = "Nháº­p cÃ¡ch dÃ¹ng";
            instructions.className = "form-control form-control-sm";
            instructions.setAttribute("aria-label", `CÃ¡ch dÃ¹ng ${medicine.name}`);
            instructions.addEventListener("input", () => {
                medicine.editInstructions = instructions.value;
            });

            instructionCell.append(instructions);
            row.append(instructionCell);

            const actionCell = document.createElement("td");
            const actionGroup = document.createElement("div");
            actionGroup.className = "doctor-medicine-actions";
            const edit = document.createElement("button");
            edit.type = "button";
            edit.className = "btn btn-outline-primary btn-sm";
            edit.dataset.editMedicine = medicine.code;
            edit.textContent = medicine.editing ? "LÆ°u" : "Chá»‰nh sá»­a";
            const remove = document.createElement("button");
            remove.type = "button";
            remove.className = "btn btn-outline-danger btn-sm";
            remove.textContent = "XÃ³a";
            remove.setAttribute("aria-label", `XÃ³a ${medicine.name} khá»i Ä‘Æ¡n`);
            remove.dataset.removeMedicine = medicine.code;
            actionGroup.append(edit, remove);
            actionCell.append(actionGroup);
            row.append(actionCell);
            prescriptionItems.append(row);
        });
    }

    function henTuDongLuuBanNhap() {
        if (!selectedPatientCode) return;
        draftDirty = true;
        draftRevision += 1;
        window.clearTimeout(autosaveTimer);
        autosaveTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
            autosaveInFlight = autosaveInFlight.then(() => {
                if (!draftDirty) return true;
                autosaveRequestActive = true;
                return lapPhieuKham(null, "NHAP", { silent: true }).finally(() => {
                    autosaveRequestActive = false;
                });
            });
        }, 500);
    }

    document.querySelector("#symptoms").addEventListener("input", henTuDongLuuBanNhap);
    document.querySelector("#diagnosis").addEventListener("input", henTuDongLuuBanNhap);
    window.addEventListener("pagehide", () => {
        window.clearTimeout(autosaveTimer);
        if (!draftDirty || !selectedPatientCode
            || autosaveRequestActive
            || Array.from(selectedMedicines.values()).some(item => item.editing)) {
            return;
        }
        autosaveRequestActive = true;
        autosaveInFlight = lapPhieuKham(null, "NHAP", {
            silent: true, keepalive: true,
        }).finally(() => {
            autosaveRequestActive = false;
        });
    });

    document.addEventListener("click", event => {
        const draftsButton = event.target.closest("[data-toggle-drafts]");
        const searchButton = event.target.closest("[data-patient-search]");
        const historyButton = event.target.closest("[data-patient-history]");
        const historyPageButton = event.target.closest("[data-history-page]");
        const historyPrescriptionsButton = event.target.closest("[data-history-prescriptions]");
        const medicineButton = event.target.closest("[data-medicine-search]");
        const medicinePageButton = event.target.closest("[data-medicine-page]");
        const addButton = event.target.closest("[data-add-medicine]");
        const editMedicineButton = event.target.closest("[data-edit-medicine]");
        const removeMedicineButton = event.target.closest("[data-remove-medicine]");
        const resumeButton = event.target.closest("[data-resume-draft]");
        const deleteDraftButton = event.target.closest("[data-delete-draft]");
        const saveDraftButton = event.target.closest("[data-save-draft]");
        const saveExamButton = event.target.closest("[data-save-exam]");
        if (draftsButton) {
            const isOpening = draftsPanel.hidden;
            draftsPanel.hidden = !isOpening;
            draftsButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpening));
            draftsButton.classList.toggle("doctor-drafts-open", isOpening);
            taiDanhSachBanNhap();
            if (isOpening) {
                draftsPanel.scrollIntoView({ block: "start", behavior: "smooth" });
            }
            return;
        }
        if (searchButton) {
            layThongTinBenhNhan();
            return;
        }
        if (historyButton) {
            layDanhSachSachLichSu();
            return;
        }
        if (historyPageButton && !historyPageButton.disabled) {
            layDanhSachSachLichSu(Number(historyPageButton.dataset.historyPage));
            return;
        }
        if (historyPrescriptionsButton) {
            doiHienThiDonThuocLichSu(historyPrescriptionsButton);
            return;
        }
        if (medicineButton) {
            traCuuThuoc(1);
            return;
        }
        if (medicinePageButton && !medicinePageButton.disabled) {
            traCuuThuoc(Number(medicinePageButton.dataset.medicinePage));
            return;
        }
        if (resumeButton) {
            tiepTucBanNhap(resumeButton.dataset.resumeDraft);
            return;
        }
        if (deleteDraftButton) {
            xoaBanNhap(deleteDraftButton.dataset.deleteDraft, deleteDraftButton);
            return;
        }
        if (editMedicineButton) {
            const medicine = selectedMedicines.get(editMedicineButton.dataset.editMedicine);
            if (!medicine) return;
            if (medicine.editing) {
                const quantity = medicine.editQuantity;
                const instructions = medicine.editInstructions ?? "";
                if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 1 || quantity > medicine.stock
                    || instructions.length > 255) {
                    hienThiTrangThaiBacSi("Sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng pháº£i náº±m trong tá»“n kho vÃ  cÃ¡ch dÃ¹ng khÃ´ng vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ 255 kÃ½ tá»±.", true);
                    return;
                }
                medicine.quantity = quantity;
                medicine.instructions = instructions.trim();
                delete medicine.editQuantity;
                delete medicine.editInstructions;
                medicine.editing = false;
                henTuDongLuuBanNhap();
            } else {
                medicine.editQuantity = medicine.quantity;
                medicine.editInstructions = medicine.instructions;
                medicine.editing = true;
            }
            hienThiDonThuoc();
            return;
        }
        if (removeMedicineButton) {
            selectedMedicines.delete(removeMedicineButton.dataset.removeMedicine);
            hienThiDonThuoc();
            henTuDongLuuBanNhap();
            return;
        }
        if (addButton) {
            const row = addButton.closest("tr");
            const cells = row.querySelectorAll("td");
            const code = addButton.dataset.addMedicine;
            if (!selectedMedicines.has(code)) {
                selectedMedicines.set(code, {
                    code,
                    name: cells[1].textContent,
                    unit: cells[2].textContent,
                    stock: Number(cells[3].textContent),
                    quantity: 1,
                    instructions: "",
                    editing: false,
                });
                hienThiDonThuoc();
                henTuDongLuuBanNhap();
            }
            return;
        }
        if (saveDraftButton) {
            lapPhieuKham(saveDraftButton, "NHAP");
            return;
        }
        if (saveExamButton) lapPhieuKham(saveExamButton, "DA_LAP");
    });

    async function lapPhieuKham(button, status, { silent = false, keepalive = false } = {}) {
        if (status === "DA_LAP" || (status === "NHAP" && !silent)) {
            window.clearTimeout(autosaveTimer);
            await autosaveInFlight;
            window.clearTimeout(autosaveTimer);
        }
        if (!selectedPatientCode) {
            if (!silent) hienThiTrangThaiBacSi("HÃ£y tÃ¬m bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n trÆ°á»›c khi lÆ°u phiáº¿u.", true);
            patientQuery.focus();
            return false;
        }
        const symptoms = document.querySelector("#symptoms").value.trim();
        const diagnosis = document.querySelector("#diagnosis").value.trim();
        if (status === "DA_LAP" && !symptoms) {
            if (!silent) hienThiTrangThaiBacSi("Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p triá»‡u chá»©ng trÆ°á»›c khi láº­p phiáº¿u.", true);
            document.querySelector("#symptoms").focus();
            return false;
        }
        if (status === "DA_LAP" && !diagnosis) {
            if (!silent) hienThiTrangThaiBacSi("Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p dá»± Ä‘oÃ¡n loáº¡i bá»‡nh trÆ°á»›c khi láº­p phiáº¿u.", true);
            document.querySelector("#diagnosis").focus();
            return false;
        }
        const prescriptions = Array.from(selectedMedicines.values());
        if (prescriptions.some(item => item.editing)) {
            if (!silent) {
                hienThiTrangThaiBacSi("HÃ£y báº¥m â€œLÆ°uâ€ á»Ÿ tá»«ng dÃ²ng thuá»‘c Ä‘ang chá»‰nh sá»­a trÆ°á»›c khi lÆ°u phiáº¿u.", true);
            }
            return false;
        }
        if (prescriptions.some(item =>
            !Number.isInteger(item.quantity) || item.quantity < 1
            || item.quantity > item.stock
            || (status === "DA_LAP" && !item.instructions.trim())
        )) {
            if (!silent) {
                hienThiTrangThaiBacSi("Kiá»ƒm tra láº¡i sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng tá»“n kho vÃ  cÃ¡ch dÃ¹ng cá»§a tá»«ng thuá»‘c.", true);
            }
            return false;
        }
        if (button) button.disabled = true;
        if (status === "NHAP") autosaveRequestActive = true;
        const requestRevision = draftRevision;
        const requestPatientCode = selectedPatientCode;
        const requestDraftId = currentDraftId;
        try {
            const response = await fetch("/doctor/api/exams", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: {
                    "Content-Type": "application/json",
                    "X-CSRF-Token": document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content,
                },
                keepalive,
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    patient_code: requestPatientCode,
                    symptoms,
                    diagnosis,
                    status,
                    draft_id: requestDraftId,
                    prescriptions: prescriptions.map(item => ({
                        medicine_code: item.code,
                        quantity: item.quantity,
                        instructions: item.instructions.trim(),
                    })),
                }),
            });
            const result = await docJson(response);
            if (status === "DA_LAP") {
                if (requestPatientCode === selectedPatientCode) {
                    datMaBanNhapHienTai(null);
                    draftDirty = false;
                    document.querySelector("#symptoms").value = "";
                    document.querySelector("#diagnosis").value = "";
                    selectedMedicines.clear();
                    hienThiDonThuoc();
                }
            } else if (requestPatientCode === selectedPatientCode) {
                datMaBanNhapHienTai(result.exam_id);
                if (requestRevision === draftRevision) {
                    draftDirty = false;
                } else {
                    henTuDongLuuBanNhap();
                }
            }
            if (!keepalive) {
                hienThiTrangThaiBacSi(
                    silent ? `ÄÃ£ tá»± lÆ°u báº£n nhÃ¡p ${result.exam_id}.`
                        : `${result.message} MÃ£ phiáº¿u: ${result.exam_id}`
                            + (result.invoice_id ? ` Â· MÃ£ hÃ³a Ä‘Æ¡n: ${result.invoice_id}` : "")
                );
                if (requestPatientCode === selectedPatientCode) {
                    await layDanhSachSachLichSu();
                }
                await taiDanhSachBanNhap();
            }
            return true;
        } catch (error) {
            if (!keepalive) hienThiTrangThaiBacSi(error.message, true);
            return false;
        } finally {
            if (button) button.disabled = false;
            if (status === "NHAP") autosaveRequestActive = false;
        }
    }

    taiDanhSachBanNhap();
    khoiPhucBanNhapDangLam();
    traCuuThuoc();
    medicineQuery.addEventListener("input", henTimKiemThuoc);

    document.addEventListener("keydown", event => {
        if (event.key !== "Enter") return;
        if (event.target === patientQuery) {
            event.preventDefault();
            layThongTinBenhNhan();
        } else if (event.target === medicineQuery) {
            event.preventDefault();
            traCuuThuoc();
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener("click", event => {
        const button = event.target.closest("[data-doctor-prototype]");
        if (!button) return;
        hienThiTrangThaiBacSi(button.dataset.doctorPrototype);
        doctorStatus.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest", behavior: "smooth" });
    });
}

    button.setAttribute("aria-label", show ? "áº¨n máº­t kháº©u" : "Hiá»‡n máº­t kháº©u");

});

    function todayValue() {
        const now = new Date();
        return [
            now.getFullYear(),
            String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
            String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
        ].join("-");
    }

    function showStatus(message, kind = "info") {
        status.textContent = message;
        status.classList.toggle("is-error", kind === "error");
        status.classList.toggle("is-success", kind === "success");
        status.hidden = !message;
    }

    function normalize(value) {
        return value.toLocaleLowerCase("vi")
            .normalize("NFD")
            .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
            .replace(/Ä‘/g, "d");
    }

    function formatDate(value) {
        const [year, month, day] = value.split("-").map(Number);
        return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN").format(new Date(year, month - 1, day));
    }

    async function requestApi(url, options = {}) {
        const response = await fetch(url, {
            ...options,
            headers: {
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                "X-CSRF-Token": csrfToken,
                ...options.headers,
            },
        });
        let data;
        try {
            data = await response.json();
        } catch {
            throw new Error("MÃ¡y chá»§ tráº£ vá» dá»¯ liá»‡u khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.");
        }
        if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(data.error || "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ hoÃ n thÃ nh thao tÃ¡c.");
        }
        return data;
    }

    async function refreshDay() {

            ? `CÃ²n ${data.waiting.length} lÆ°á»£t chá» cÅ© chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c chuyá»ƒn lá»‹ch. HÃ£y liÃªn há»‡ quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn.`
            : "";
        dayConfirmed = data.confirmed;
        dayLocked = data.locked;
        nurseApp.dataset.maxPatients = String(dailyLimit);
        limitLabel.textContent = `Giá»›i háº¡n: ${dailyLimit} bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n/ngÃ y`;

    }

    function clearPatientLookup() {

    async function searchPatientsByCode(query, requestId) {
        if (dayLocked) return;
        try {
            const url = new URL("/nurse/api/patients", window.location.origin);
            url.searchParams.set("q", query);

                renderPatientSuggestions(data.patients);
            }
        } catch (error) {

    function addPagination(container, pageCount, page, action) {
        container.replaceChildren();
        if (pageCount <= 1) return;

        function addPageButton(label, target, disabled = false, current = false) {
            const button = document.createElement("button");
            button.type = "button";
            button.className = current
                ? "btn btn-sm btn-primary"
                : "btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary";
            button.textContent = label;
            button.dataset[action] = String(target);
            button.disabled = disabled;
            if (current) button.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
            container.append(button);
        }

        addPageButton("< TrÆ°á»›c", page - 1, page === 1);
        for (let target = 1; target <= pageCount; target += 1) {
            addPageButton(String(target), target, false, target === page);
        }
        addPageButton("Sau >", page + 1, page === pageCount);
    }

    function filtered(items) {
        const query = normalize(searchTerm.trim());
        return items.filter(patient => (
            !query || normalize(patient.name).includes(query)
        ));
    }

    async function huyLuotKham(listId, patientId) {
        const patient = activePatients
            .find(item => (
                String(item.listId) === String(listId)
                && String(item.patientId) === String(patientId)
            ));
        if (!patient || !window.confirm(`Há»§y lÆ°á»£t khÃ¡m cá»§a ${patient.name}?`)) return;
        try {
            const result = await goiApi(
                `/nurse/api/lists/${patient.listId}/${patient.patientId}`, {
                method: "DELETE",
                }
            );
            await taiLaiDanhSachNgay();
            hienThiTrangThai(result.message, "success");
        } catch (error) {
            showStatus(error.message, "error");
        }
    }
