"""One-time UUID-to-integer migration, run only with application writers stopped."""

from sqlalchemy import (
    Column, Date, DateTime, Enum, ForeignKey, ForeignKeyConstraint, Index,
    Integer, MetaData, String, Table, UniqueConstraint, inspect, select, text,
)
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine

from app import db


TABLE_NAMES = ("TaiKhoan", "NhanVien", "BenhNhan", "BacSi")
ID_COLUMNS = {"TaiKhoan": "MaTK", "NhanVien": "MaNV", "BenhNhan": "MaBN", "BacSi": "MaNV"}
STAGE = "_integer_stage"
BACKUP = "_uuid_backup"
NURSE_SLOT_INDEX = "uq_dskham_ngay_thu_tu"
NURSE_DAY_INDEX = "ix_dskham_ngay_trang_thai"
NURSE_DETAIL_PROFILE_COLUMNS = ("HoTen", "GioiTinh", "NamSinh", "DiaChi")
NURSE_DETAIL_REQUIRED_COLUMNS = {
    "MaDSKham", "MaBN", "NgayKham",
    "SoThuTu", "NgayDangKy", "TrangThai",
}
DON_VI_THUOC_CU = {"gÃ³i", "Ä‘Æ¡n vá»‹ kiá»ƒm thá»­"}


def chuyenDonViThuocVeBaLoai(engine: Engine) -> bool:
    if engine.dialect.name not in {"mysql", "mariadb", "sqlite"}:
        raise ValueError("This migration supports MySQL/TiDB and SQLite only.")
    if not {"DonViThuoc", "Thuoc"}.issubset(inspect(engine).get_table_names()):
        raise ValueError("DonViThuoc and Thuoc are required; run init-db first.")

    don_vi_chuan = (
        ("SAMPLE-VIEN", "ViÃªn"),
        ("SAMPLE-CHAI", "Chai"),
        ("SAMPLE-VI", "Vá»‰"),
    )
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        don_vis = connection.execute(
            text("SELECT MaDonVi, tenDonVi FROM DonViThuoc ORDER BY MaDonVi")
        ).mappings().all()
        ten_hop_le = {ten.casefold() for _, ten in don_vi_chuan}
        ten_cu = {row["tenDonVi"].strip().casefold() for row in don_vis}
        ten_khong_ro = ten_cu - ten_hop_le - DON_VI_THUOC_CU
        if ten_khong_ro:
            raise ValueError(
                "Unmapped existing medicine unit(s): "
                + ", ".join(sorted(ten_khong_ro))
                + ". Add an explicit mapping before retrying."
            )

        don_vis_theo_ten = {}
        for don_vi in don_vis:
            don_vis_theo_ten.setdefault(don_vi["tenDonVi"].strip().casefold(), []).append(
                don_vi["MaDonVi"]
            )

        ma_dich = {}
        ma_dang_dung = {row["MaDonVi"] for row in don_vis}
        da_thay_doi = False
        for ma_mac_dinh, ten_chuan in don_vi_chuan:
            ten_khoa = ten_chuan.casefold()
            ma_cac_dong = don_vis_theo_ten.get(ten_khoa, [])
            if ma_cac_dong:
                ma_dich[ten_khoa] = ma_cac_dong[0]
            else:
                ma_moi = ma_mac_dinh
                hau_to = 1
                while ma_moi in ma_dang_dung:
                    ma_moi = f"{ma_mac_dinh}-{hau_to}"
                    hau_to += 1
                connection.execute(
                    text(
                        "INSERT INTO DonViThuoc (MaDonVi, tenDonVi) "
                        "VALUES (:ma, :ten)"
                    ),
                    {"ma": ma_moi, "ten": ten_chuan},
                )
                ma_dang_dung.add(ma_moi)
                ma_dich[ten_khoa] = ma_moi
                da_thay_doi = True

        ma_vien = ma_dich["viÃªn"]
        for don_vi in don_vis:
            ma_cu = don_vi["MaDonVi"]
            ten_cu = don_vi["tenDonVi"].strip().casefold()
            ma_moi = ma_dich.get(ten_cu, ma_vien)
            ten_chuan = next(
                (ten for _, ten in don_vi_chuan if ten.casefold() == ten_cu), None
            )
            if ma_cu != ma_moi:
                connection.execute(
                    text("UPDATE Thuoc SET MaDonVi = :ma_moi WHERE MaDonVi = :ma_cu"),
                    {"ma_moi": ma_moi, "ma_cu": ma_cu},
                )
                connection.execute(
                    text("DELETE FROM DonViThuoc WHERE MaDonVi = :ma_cu"),
                    {"ma_cu": ma_cu},
                )
                da_thay_doi = True
            elif ten_chuan is not None and don_vi["tenDonVi"] != ten_chuan:
                connection.execute(
                    text("UPDATE DonViThuoc SET tenDonVi = :ten WHERE MaDonVi = :ma"),
                    {"ten": ten_chuan, "ma": ma_cu},
                )
                da_thay_doi = True

        return da_thay_doi


def xoaCotHoSoTrung(engine: Engine) -> bool:
    if engine.dialect.name not in {"mysql", "mariadb", "sqlite"}:
        raise ValueError("This migration supports MySQL/TiDB and SQLite only.")
    if "ChiTietDanhSachKham" not in inspect(engine).get_table_names():
        raise ValueError("ChiTietDanhSachKham is missing; run init-db first.")

    def xoaCacCot(connection) -> bool:
        columns = {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspect(connection).get_columns("ChiTietDanhSachKham")
        }
        missing = NURSE_DETAIL_REQUIRED_COLUMNS - columns
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(
                "ChiTietDanhSachKham has an incomplete schema; inspect before retrying."
            )
        quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
        changed = False
        for column in NURSE_DETAIL_PROFILE_COLUMNS:
            if column in columns:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"ALTER TABLE {quote('ChiTietDanhSachKham')} "
                    f"DROP COLUMN {quote(column)}"
                )
                changed = True
        return changed

    if engine.dialect.name != "sqlite":
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            return xoaCacCot(connection)

    with engine.connect() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=OFF")
        connection.commit()
        try:
            with connection.begin():
                changed = xoaCacCot(connection)
        finally:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
            connection.commit()
        violations = connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").all()
        if violations:
            raise ValueError(
                "Foreign-key violations found after migration; inspect the database."
            )
        return changed


def chuyenMaTaiKhoan(engine: Engine) -> bool:
    if engine.dialect.name not in {"mysql", "sqlite"}:
        raise ValueError("This migration supports MySQL/TiDB and SQLite only.")
    inspector = inspect(engine)
    names = set(inspector.get_table_names())
    if not set(TABLE_NAMES).issubset(names):
        raise ValueError("All four existing account/profile tables are required.")
    integer_tables = {
        name for name in TABLE_NAMES
        if any(c["name"] == ID_COLUMNS[name] and isinstance(c["type"], Integer)
               for c in inspector.get_columns(name))
    }
    if integer_tables == set(TABLE_NAMES):
        return False
    if integer_tables:
        raise ValueError("Mixed identifier schema detected; manual inspection required.")
    if any(name + suffix in names for name in TABLE_NAMES for suffix in (STAGE, BACKUP)):
        raise ValueError("Migration staging/backup tables already exist; inspect before retrying.")
    for name in names - set(TABLE_NAMES):
        if any(fk["referred_table"] in TABLE_NAMES for fk in inspector.get_foreign_keys(name)):
            raise ValueError("Other tables reference account/profile IDs; extend the migration first.")

    original = MetaData()
    original.reflect(engine, only=TABLE_NAMES)
    for name in TABLE_NAMES:
        if set(original.tables[name].columns.keys()) != set(db.metadata.tables[name].columns.keys()):
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected columns in {name}; refusing to discard data.")

    staging = MetaData()
    for name in TABLE_NAMES:
        model_table = db.metadata.tables[name]
        columns = []
        for column in model_table.columns:
            foreign_keys = [
                ForeignKey(f"{fk.column.table.name}{STAGE}.{fk.column.name}")
                for fk in column.foreign_keys
            ]
            column_type = column.type.copy()
            if isinstance(column_type, Enum) and column_type.name:
                column_type.name += STAGE
            columns.append(Column(
                column.name, column_type, *foreign_keys, primary_key=column.primary_key,
                nullable=column.nullable, unique=column.unique, autoincrement=column.autoincrement,
            ))
        Table(
            name + STAGE, staging, *columns,
            sqlite_autoincrement=model_table.dialect_options["sqlite"].get("autoincrement", False),
        )

    with engine.begin() as connection:
        if engine.dialect.name == "sqlite":
            connection.exec_driver_sql("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        source = {
            name: [dict(row) for row in connection.execute(
                select(original.tables[name]).order_by(original.tables[name].c[ID_COLUMNS[name]])
            ).mappings()]
            for name in TABLE_NAMES
        }
        account_ids = {row["MaTK"]: i for i, row in enumerate(source["TaiKhoan"], 1)}
        employee_ids = {row["MaNV"]: i for i, row in enumerate(source["NhanVien"], 1)}
        converted = {}
        for name in TABLE_NAMES:
            rows = []
            for i, row in enumerate(source[name], 1):
                new_row = row.copy()
                if name == "TaiKhoan":
                    new_row["MaTK"] = account_ids[row["MaTK"]]
                elif name == "BacSi":
                    new_row["MaNV"] = employee_ids[row["MaNV"]]
                else:
                    new_row[ID_COLUMNS[name]] = i
                    new_row["MaTK"] = account_ids[row["MaTK"]]
                rows.append(new_row)
            converted[name] = rows

        staging.create_all(connection)
        for name in TABLE_NAMES:
            table = staging.tables[name + STAGE]
            rows = converted[name]
            if rows:
                connection.execute(table.insert(), rows)
            actual = [dict(row) for row in connection.execute(
                select(table).order_by(table.c[ID_COLUMNS[name]])
            ).mappings()]
            if actual != sorted(rows, key=lambda row: row[ID_COLUMNS[name]]):
                raise ValueError(f"Copied rows do not match in {name}; original tables unchanged.")

        for name in TABLE_NAMES:
            current = [dict(row) for row in connection.execute(
                select(original.tables[name]).order_by(original.tables[name].c[ID_COLUMNS[name]])
            ).mappings()]
            if current != source[name]:
                raise ValueError("Source changed during migration; stop all writers before retrying.")

        quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
        if engine.dialect.name == "mysql":
            pairs = []
            for name in TABLE_NAMES:
                pairs.extend((
                    f"{quote(name)} TO {quote(name + BACKUP)}",
                    f"{quote(name + STAGE)} TO {quote(name)}",
                ))
            connection.exec_driver_sql("RENAME TABLE " + ", ".join(pairs))
        else:
            for name in TABLE_NAMES:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"ALTER TABLE {quote(name)} RENAME TO {quote(name + BACKUP)}"
                )
            for name in TABLE_NAMES:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"ALTER TABLE {quote(name + STAGE)} RENAME TO {quote(name)}"
                )
    return True


def _choPhepBenhNhanKhongCoTaiKhoanSqlite(connection) -> bool:
    columns = {column["name"]: column for column in inspect(connection).get_columns("BenhNhan")}
    if "MaTK" not in columns:
        raise ValueError("Unexpected BenhNhan schema; inspect it before retrying.")
    if columns["MaTK"]["nullable"]:
        return False
    expected = {"MaBN", "HoTen", "GioiTinh", "NamSinh", "DiaChi", "SoDienThoai", "MaTK"}
    if set(columns) != expected:
        raise ValueError("BenhNhan has unexpected columns; inspect it before retrying.")

    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        'CREATE TABLE "BenhNhan_nurse_tmp" ('
        '"MaBN" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, '
        '"HoTen" VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, '
        '"GioiTinh" VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL, '
        '"NamSinh" INTEGER NOT NULL, '
        '"DiaChi" VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, '
        '"SoDienThoai" VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, '
        '"MaTK" INTEGER, '
        'FOREIGN KEY ("MaTK") REFERENCES "TaiKhoan" ("MaTK"), '
        'UNIQUE ("MaTK"))'
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        'INSERT INTO "BenhNhan_nurse_tmp" '
        '("MaBN", "HoTen", "GioiTinh", "NamSinh", "DiaChi", "SoDienThoai", "MaTK") '
        'SELECT "MaBN", "HoTen", "GioiTinh", "NamSinh", "DiaChi", "SoDienThoai", "MaTK" '
        'FROM "BenhNhan"'
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql('DROP TABLE "BenhNhan"')
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        'ALTER TABLE "BenhNhan_nurse_tmp" RENAME TO "BenhNhan"'
    )
    return True


def _nangCapCauTrucYTa(connection, engine: Engine) -> bool:
    inspector = inspect(connection)
    tables = set(inspector.get_table_names())
    required = {"TaiKhoan", "BenhNhan", "DanhSachKham", "QuyDinh"}
    missing = required - tables
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(
            "Required tables are missing; run init-db first: " + ", ".join(sorted(missing))
        )
    if "ChiTietDanhSachKham" in tables:
        detail_columns = {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspector.get_columns("ChiTietDanhSachKham")
        }
        day_columns = {
            column["name"] for column in inspector.get_columns("DanhSachKham")
        }
        detail_primary_key = inspector.get_pk_constraint(
            "ChiTietDanhSachKham"
        ).get("constrained_columns")
        has_registration_identity = (
            detail_primary_key == ["MaDSKham", "MaBN"]
            or (
                detail_primary_key == ["MaCTDSKham"]
                and "MaCTDSKham" in detail_columns
            )
        )
        if (
            {"MaDSKham", "MaBN", "NgayKham", "SoThuTu", "TrangThai"}
            <= detail_columns
            and has_registration_identity
            and {"MaDSKham", "NgayKham", "MaNV"} <= day_columns
        ):
            return False
        raise ValueError(
            "ChiTietDanhSachKham exists with an incomplete schema; inspect before retrying."
        )

    changed = False
    if engine.dialect.name == "sqlite":
        changed |= _choPhepBenhNhanKhongCoTaiKhoanSqlite(connection)
    else:
        patient_columns = {
            column["name"]: column
            for column in inspector.get_columns("BenhNhan")
        }
        account_column = patient_columns.get("MaTK")
        if account_column is None:
            raise ValueError("BenhNhan.MaTK is missing; inspect the schema before retrying.")
        if not account_column["nullable"]:
            quote = engine.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
            column_type = str(account_column["type"])
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"ALTER TABLE {quote('BenhNhan')} MODIFY COLUMN "
                f"{quote('MaTK')} {column_type} NULL"
            )
            changed = True

    registration_columns = {
        column["name"] for column in inspect(connection).get_columns("DanhSachKham")
    }
    quote = engine.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
    if "NgayKham" not in registration_columns:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"ALTER TABLE {quote('DanhSachKham')} ADD COLUMN {quote('NgayKham')} DATE NULL"
        )
        changed = True
    if "SoThuTu" not in registration_columns:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"ALTER TABLE {quote('DanhSachKham')} ADD COLUMN {quote('SoThuTu')} INTEGER NULL"
        )
        changed = True

    connection.execute(text(
        f"UPDATE {quote('DanhSachKham')} SET {quote('NgayKham')} = "
        f"DATE({quote('NgayDangKy')}) WHERE {quote('NgayKham')} IS NULL"
    ))
    unresolved_dates = connection.execute(text(
        f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {quote('DanhSachKham')} "
        f"WHERE {quote('NgayKham')} IS NULL"
    )).scalar_one()
    if unresolved_dates:
        raise ValueError("Some registrations have no valid registration date; inspect them first.")

    rows = connection.execute(text(
        f"SELECT {quote('MaDSKham')}, {quote('NgayKham')}, {quote('SoThuTu')}, "
        f"{quote('TrangThai')} FROM {quote('DanhSachKham')} ORDER BY "
        f"{quote('NgayKham')}, {quote('NgayDangKy')}, {quote('MaDSKham')}"
    )).mappings().all()
    used_slots: dict[object, set[int]] = {}
    for row in rows:
        exam_date = row["NgayKham"]
        day_slots = used_slots.setdefault(exam_date, set())
        slot = row["SoThuTu"]
        if row["TrangThai"] not in {"CHO_KHAM", "DA_XAC_NHAN"}:
            if slot is not None:
                connection.execute(
                    text(
                        f"UPDATE {quote('DanhSachKham')} SET {quote('SoThuTu')} = NULL "
                        f"WHERE {quote('MaDSKham')} = :id"
                    ),
                    {"id": row["MaDSKham"]},
                )
                changed = True
            continue
        if slot is None or slot in day_slots:
            slot = 1
            while slot in day_slots:
                slot += 1
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    f"UPDATE {quote('DanhSachKham')} SET {quote('SoThuTu')} = :slot "
                    f"WHERE {quote('MaDSKham')} = :id"
                ),
                {"slot": slot, "id": row["MaDSKham"]},
            )
            changed = True
        day_slots.add(slot)

    if engine.dialect.name in {"mysql", "mariadb"}:
        day_column = next(
            column for column in inspect(connection).get_columns("DanhSachKham")
            if column["name"] == "NgayKham"
        )
        if day_column["nullable"]:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"ALTER TABLE {quote('DanhSachKham')} MODIFY COLUMN "
                f"{quote('NgayKham')} DATE NOT NULL"
            )
            changed = True

    indexes = {
        index["name"]: index for index in inspect(connection).get_indexes("DanhSachKham")
    }
    if NURSE_SLOT_INDEX not in indexes:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"CREATE UNIQUE INDEX {quote(NURSE_SLOT_INDEX)} "
            f"ON {quote('DanhSachKham')} ({quote('NgayKham')}, {quote('SoThuTu')})"
        )
        changed = True
    elif not indexes[NURSE_SLOT_INDEX]["unique"]:
        raise ValueError("The nurse slot index exists but is not unique; inspect it first.")
    if NURSE_DAY_INDEX not in indexes:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"CREATE INDEX {quote(NURSE_DAY_INDEX)} "
            f"ON {quote('DanhSachKham')} ({quote('NgayKham')}, {quote('TrangThai')})"
        )
        changed = True

    if connection.execute(
        text(f"SELECT 1 FROM {quote('QuyDinh')} WHERE {quote('MaQD')} = :code"),
        {"code": "SO_BENH_NHAN_TOI_DA"},
    ).first() is None:
        connection.execute(
            text(
                f"INSERT INTO {quote('QuyDinh')} "
                f"({quote('MaQD')}, {quote('TenQuyDinh')}, {quote('GiaTri')}) "
                "VALUES (:code, :name, :value)"
            ),
            {
                "code": "SO_BENH_NHAN_TOI_DA",
                "name": "Sá»‘ bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n tá»‘i Ä‘a trong ngÃ y",
                "value": 40,
            },
        )
        changed = True
    return changed


def nangCapCauTrucHangCho(engine: Engine) -> bool:
    if engine.dialect.name not in {"mysql", "mariadb", "sqlite"}:
        raise ValueError("This migration supports MySQL/TiDB and SQLite only.")
    if engine.dialect.name != "sqlite":
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            return _nangCapCauTrucYTa(connection, engine)

    with engine.connect() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=OFF")
        connection.commit()
        try:
            with connection.begin():
                changed = _nangCapCauTrucYTa(connection, engine)
        finally:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
            connection.commit()
        violations = connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").all()
        if violations:
            raise ValueError("Foreign-key violations found after migration; inspect the database.")
        return changed


def nangCapCauTrucDanhSachKham(engine: Engine) -> bool:
    if engine.dialect.name not in {"mysql", "mariadb", "sqlite"}:
        raise ValueError("This migration supports MySQL/TiDB and SQLite only.")

    tables = set(inspect(engine).get_table_names())
    if "DanhSachKham" not in tables or "BenhNhan" not in tables or "NhanVien" not in tables:
        raise ValueError(
            "DanhSachKham, BenhNhan, and NhanVien are required; run init-db first."
        )
    if "ChiTietDanhSachKham" in tables:
        day_columns = {
            column["name"] for column in inspect(engine).get_columns("DanhSachKham")
        }
        detail_columns = {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspect(engine).get_columns("ChiTietDanhSachKham")
        }
        detail_primary_key = inspect(engine).get_pk_constraint(
            "ChiTietDanhSachKham"
        ).get("constrained_columns")
        has_registration_identity = (
            detail_primary_key == ["MaDSKham", "MaBN"]
            or (
                detail_primary_key == ["MaCTDSKham"]
                and "MaCTDSKham" in detail_columns
            )
        )
        if (
            {"MaDSKham", "NgayKham", "MaNV"} <= day_columns
            and {
                "MaDSKham", "MaBN", "NgayKham",
                "SoThuTu", "NgayDangKy", "TrangThai",
            } <= detail_columns
            and has_registration_identity
        ):
            return False
        raise ValueError(
            "ChiTietDanhSachKham exists with an incomplete schema; inspect before retrying."
        )

    stage_day_name = "_DanhSachKham_m2m"
    stage_detail_name = "_ChiTietDanhSachKham_m2m"
    backup_name = "DanhSachKham_legacy"
    if {stage_day_name, stage_detail_name, backup_name} & tables:
        raise ValueError(
            "Migration staging or backup tables already exist; inspect before retrying."
        )

    day_columns = {
        column["name"] for column in inspect(engine).get_columns("DanhSachKham")
    }
    if "MaBN" not in day_columns:
        raise ValueError(
            "DanhSachKham does not match the legacy registration schema; inspect it first."
        )

    nangCapCauTrucHangCho(engine)
    source_metadata = MetaData()
    source_day = Table("DanhSachKham", source_metadata, autoload_with=engine)
    expected_source_columns = {
        "MaDSKham", "MaBN", "HoTen", "GioiTinh", "NamSinh", "DiaChi",
        "NgayKham", "SoThuTu", "NgayDangKy", "TrangThai",
    }
    if not expected_source_columns <= set(source_day.columns.keys()):
        raise ValueError(
            "DanhSachKham has unexpected columns; inspect it before migration."
        )

    staging_metadata = MetaData()
    Table("NhanVien", staging_metadata, autoload_with=engine)
    Table("BenhNhan", staging_metadata, autoload_with=engine)
    staged_day = Table(
        stage_day_name,
        staging_metadata,
        Column("MaDSKham", Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True),
        Column("NgayKham", Date, nullable=False),
        Column("MaNV", Integer, ForeignKey("NhanVien.MaNV"), nullable=True),
        UniqueConstraint("NgayKham", name="uq_dskham_ngay"),
        UniqueConstraint("MaDSKham", "NgayKham", name="uq_dskham_id_ngay"),
        sqlite_autoincrement=True,
    )
    staged_detail = Table(
        stage_detail_name,
        staging_metadata,
        Column("MaDSKham", Integer, primary_key=True),
        Column("MaBN", Integer, ForeignKey("BenhNhan.MaBN"), primary_key=True),
        Column("NgayKham", Date, nullable=False),
        Column("SoThuTu", Integer, nullable=True),
        Column("NgayDangKy", DateTime, nullable=False),
        Column("TrangThai", String(20), nullable=False),
        ForeignKeyConstraint(
            ["MaDSKham", "NgayKham"],
            [f"{stage_day_name}.MaDSKham", f"{stage_day_name}.NgayKham"],
            name="fk_ctdsk_list_date",
        ),
        Index("uq_ctdsk_ngay_thu_tu", "NgayKham", "SoThuTu", unique=True),
        Index("ix_ctdsk_ngay_trang_thai", "NgayKham", "TrangThai"),
        sqlite_autoincrement=True,
    )

    def saoChepVaThayBang(connection) -> None:
        source_rows = connection.execute(
            select(source_day).order_by(source_day.c.NgayKham, source_day.c.MaDSKham)
        ).mappings().all()
        dates = sorted({row["NgayKham"] for row in source_rows})
        day_ids = {exam_date: index for index, exam_date in enumerate(dates, 1)}
        day_rows = [
            {"MaDSKham": day_ids[exam_date], "NgayKham": exam_date, "MaNV": None}
            for exam_date in dates
        ]
        detail_rows_by_pair = {}
        for row in source_rows:
            pair = (day_ids[row["NgayKham"]], row["MaBN"])
            detail_rows_by_pair[pair] = {
                "MaDSKham": pair[0],
                "MaBN": pair[1],
                "NgayKham": row["NgayKham"],
                "SoThuTu": row["SoThuTu"],
                "NgayDangKy": row["NgayDangKy"],
                "TrangThai": row["TrangThai"],
            }
        detail_rows = list(detail_rows_by_pair.values())

        staging_metadata.create_all(connection)
        if day_rows:
            connection.execute(staged_day.insert(), day_rows)
        if detail_rows:
            connection.execute(staged_detail.insert(), detail_rows)

        copied_rows = connection.execute(
            select(
                staged_detail.c.MaDSKham,
                staged_detail.c.MaBN,
                staged_detail.c.NgayKham,
                staged_detail.c.SoThuTu,
                staged_detail.c.NgayDangKy,
                staged_detail.c.TrangThai,
            ).order_by(staged_detail.c.MaDSKham, staged_detail.c.MaBN)
        ).all()
        expected_rows = [
            (
                row["MaDSKham"], row["MaBN"], row["NgayKham"],
                row["SoThuTu"], row["NgayDangKy"], row["TrangThai"],
            )
            for row in sorted(
                detail_rows,
                key=lambda item: (item["MaDSKham"], item["MaBN"]),
            )
        ]
        if copied_rows != expected_rows:
            raise ValueError(
                "Migrated registration rows do not match the source; original table retained."
            )

        quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
        if engine.dialect.name in {"mysql", "mariadb"}:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "RENAME TABLE "
                f"{quote('DanhSachKham')} TO {quote(backup_name)}, "
                f"{quote(stage_day_name)} TO {quote('DanhSachKham')}, "
                f"{quote(stage_detail_name)} TO {quote('ChiTietDanhSachKham')}"
            )
        else:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"ALTER TABLE {quote('DanhSachKham')} RENAME TO {quote(backup_name)}"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"ALTER TABLE {quote(stage_day_name)} RENAME TO {quote('DanhSachKham')}"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"ALTER TABLE {quote(stage_detail_name)} "
                f"RENAME TO {quote('ChiTietDanhSachKham')}"
            )

    if engine.dialect.name == "sqlite":
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=OFF")
            connection.commit()
            try:
                with connection.begin():
                    saoChepVaThayBang(connection)
            finally:
                connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
                connection.commit()
            violations = connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").all()
            if violations:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Foreign-key violations found after migration; inspect the database."
                )
    else:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            saoChepVaThayBang(connection)
    return True


def loaiBoNgayKhamKhoiChiTietDanhSachKham(engine: Engine) -> bool:
    """Remove the redundant date column and keep only the list/patient identity."""
    if engine.dialect.name not in {"mysql", "mariadb", "sqlite"}:
        raise ValueError("This migration supports MySQL/TiDB and SQLite only.")

    table_name = "ChiTietDanhSachKham"
    inspector = inspect(engine)
    if table_name not in inspector.get_table_names():
        raise ValueError("ChiTietDanhSachKham is missing; run init-db first.")

    columns = {column["name"] for column in inspector.get_columns(table_name)}
    if "NgayKham" not in columns:
        return False

    required = {
        "MaDSKham", "MaBN", "NgayKham",
        "SoThuTu", "NgayDangKy", "TrangThai",
    }
    if not required <= columns:
        raise ValueError(
            "ChiTietDanhSachKham has an unexpected schema; inspect it before migration."
        )

    quote = engine.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote

    if engine.dialect.name in {"mysql", "mariadb"}:
        stage_name = "_ChiTietDanhSachKham_normalized"
        backup_name = "ChiTietDanhSachKham_date_backup"
        existing_tables = set(inspector.get_table_names())
        if {stage_name, backup_name} & existing_tables:
            raise ValueError(
                "Migration staging or backup table already exists; inspect before retrying."
            )
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            constraints = connection.execute(text(
                "SELECT CONSTRAINT_NAME "
                "FROM information_schema.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE "
                "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() "
                "AND TABLE_NAME = 'ChiTietDanhSachKham' "
                "AND REFERENCED_TABLE_NAME IS NOT NULL "
                "AND CONSTRAINT_NAME != 'PRIMARY'"
            )).scalars().all()
            for constraint_name in constraints:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"ALTER TABLE {quote(table_name)} "
                    f"DROP FOREIGN KEY {quote(constraint_name)}"
                )

            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"CREATE TABLE {quote(stage_name)} ("
                f"{quote('MaDSKham')} INTEGER NOT NULL,"
                f"{quote('MaBN')} INTEGER NOT NULL,"
                f"{quote('SoThuTu')} INTEGER,"
                f"{quote('NgayDangKy')} DATETIME NOT NULL,"
                f"{quote('TrangThai')} VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,"
                f"PRIMARY KEY ({quote('MaDSKham')}, {quote('MaBN')}),"
                f"UNIQUE INDEX {quote('uq_ctdsk_list_slot')} "
                f"({quote('MaDSKham')}, {quote('SoThuTu')}),"
                f"INDEX {quote('ix_ctdsk_list_status')} "
                f"({quote('MaDSKham')}, {quote('TrangThai')}),"
                f"CONSTRAINT {quote('fk_ctdsk_list')} "
                f"FOREIGN KEY ({quote('MaDSKham')}) "
                f"REFERENCES {quote('DanhSachKham')} ({quote('MaDSKham')}),"
                f"CONSTRAINT {quote('fk_ctdsk_patient')} "
                f"FOREIGN KEY ({quote('MaBN')}) "
                f"REFERENCES {quote('BenhNhan')} ({quote('MaBN')})"
                ")"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"INSERT INTO {quote(stage_name)} "
                f"({quote('MaDSKham')}, {quote('MaBN')}, "
                f"{quote('SoThuTu')}, {quote('NgayDangKy')}, {quote('TrangThai')}) "
                f"SELECT {quote('MaDSKham')}, {quote('MaBN')}, "
                f"{quote('SoThuTu')}, {quote('NgayDangKy')}, {quote('TrangThai')} "
                f"FROM {quote(table_name)}"
            )
            source_count = connection.execute(text(
                f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {quote(table_name)}"
            )).scalar_one()
            stage_count = connection.execute(text(
                f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {quote(stage_name)}"
            )).scalar_one()
            if stage_count != source_count:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Date-column removal verification failed; original table retained."
                )

            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"RENAME TABLE {quote(table_name)} TO {quote(backup_name)}, "
                f"{quote(stage_name)} TO {quote(table_name)}"
            )
        return True

    stage_name = "_ChiTietDanhSachKham_normalized"
    with engine.connect() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=OFF")
        connection.commit()
        try:
            with connection.begin():
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"CREATE TABLE {quote(stage_name)} ("
                    f"{quote('MaDSKham')} INTEGER NOT NULL,"
                    f"{quote('MaBN')} INTEGER NOT NULL,"
                    f"{quote('SoThuTu')} INTEGER,"
                    f"{quote('NgayDangKy')} DATETIME NOT NULL,"
                    f"{quote('TrangThai')} VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,"
                    "PRIMARY KEY (MaDSKham, MaBN),"
                    "CONSTRAINT fk_ctdsk_list "
                    "FOREIGN KEY (MaDSKham) REFERENCES DanhSachKham(MaDSKham),"
                    "CONSTRAINT fk_ctdsk_patient "
                    "FOREIGN KEY (MaBN) REFERENCES BenhNhan(MaBN)"
                    ")"
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"INSERT INTO {quote(stage_name)} "
                    f"({quote('MaDSKham')}, {quote('MaBN')}, {quote('SoThuTu')}, "
                    f"{quote('NgayDangKy')}, {quote('TrangThai')}) "
                    f"SELECT {quote('MaDSKham')}, {quote('MaBN')}, "
                    f"{quote('SoThuTu')}, {quote('NgayDangKy')}, {quote('TrangThai')} "
                    f"FROM {quote(table_name)}"
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"ALTER TABLE {quote(table_name)} "
                    f"RENAME TO {quote('_ChiTietDanhSachKham_backup_date')}"
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"ALTER TABLE {quote(stage_name)} "
                    f"RENAME TO {quote(table_name)}"
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"CREATE UNIQUE INDEX {quote('uq_ctdsk_list_slot')} "
                    f"ON {quote(table_name)} ({quote('MaDSKham')}, {quote('SoThuTu')})"
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"CREATE INDEX {quote('ix_ctdsk_list_status')} "
                    f"ON {quote(table_name)} ({quote('MaDSKham')}, {quote('TrangThai')})"
                )
        finally:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
            connection.commit()
    return True


def chuyenChiTietDanhSachKhamSangKhoaGhep(engine: Engine) -> bool:
    if engine.dialect.name not in {"mysql", "mariadb", "sqlite"}:
        raise ValueError("This migration supports MySQL/TiDB and SQLite only.")

    table_name = "ChiTietDanhSachKham"
    stage_name = "_ChiTietDanhSachKham_composite"
    backup_name = "ChiTietDanhSachKham_legacy"
    inspector = inspect(engine)
    if table_name not in inspector.get_table_names():
        raise ValueError("ChiTietDanhSachKham is missing; run init-db first.")

    columns = {column["name"] for column in inspector.get_columns(table_name)}
    primary_key = inspector.get_pk_constraint(table_name).get("constrained_columns")
    if primary_key == ["MaDSKham", "MaBN"] and "MaCTDSKham" not in columns:
        return False
    required = {
        "MaCTDSKham", "MaDSKham", "MaBN", "NgayKham",
        "SoThuTu", "NgayDangKy", "TrangThai",
    }
    if primary_key != ["MaCTDSKham"] or not required <= columns:
        raise ValueError(
            "ChiTietDanhSachKham has an unexpected schema; inspect it before migration."
        )
    existing_tables = set(inspector.get_table_names())
    if not {"DanhSachKham", "BenhNhan"} <= existing_tables:
        raise ValueError(
            "DanhSachKham and BenhNhan are required; run init-db first."
        )
    if {stage_name, backup_name} & existing_tables:
        raise ValueError(
            "Migration staging or backup table already exists; inspect before retrying."
        )

    source_metadata = MetaData()
    source = Table(
        table_name,
        source_metadata,
        Column("MaCTDSKham", Integer),
        Column("MaDSKham", Integer),
        Column("MaBN", Integer),
        Column("NgayKham", Date),
        Column("SoThuTu", Integer),
        Column("NgayDangKy", DateTime),
        Column("TrangThai", String(20)),
    )
    staging_metadata = MetaData()
    Table(
        "DanhSachKham",
        staging_metadata,
        Column("MaDSKham", Integer, primary_key=True),
        Column("NgayKham", Date, nullable=False),
        UniqueConstraint("MaDSKham", "NgayKham", name="uq_dskham_id_ngay"),
    )
    Table(
        "BenhNhan",
        staging_metadata,
        Column("MaBN", Integer, primary_key=True),
    )
    staged = Table(
        stage_name,
        staging_metadata,
        Column("MaDSKham", Integer, primary_key=True),
        Column("MaBN", Integer, ForeignKey("BenhNhan.MaBN"), primary_key=True),
        Column("NgayKham", Date, nullable=False),
        Column("SoThuTu", Integer, nullable=True),
        Column("NgayDangKy", DateTime, nullable=False),
        Column("TrangThai", String(20), nullable=False),
        ForeignKeyConstraint(
            ["MaDSKham", "NgayKham"],
            ["DanhSachKham.MaDSKham", "DanhSachKham.NgayKham"],
            name="fk_ctdsk_list_date",
        ),
    )

    def saoChepVaThayBang(connection) -> None:
        source_rows = connection.execute(
            select(
                source.c.MaCTDSKham,
                source.c.MaDSKham,
                source.c.MaBN,
                source.c.NgayKham,
                source.c.SoThuTu,
                source.c.NgayDangKy,
                source.c.TrangThai,
            ).order_by(source.c.MaCTDSKham)
        ).mappings().all()
        latest_by_pair = {}
        for row in source_rows:
            pair = (row["MaDSKham"], row["MaBN"])
            latest_by_pair[pair] = {
                "MaDSKham": row["MaDSKham"],
                "MaBN": row["MaBN"],
                "NgayKham": row["NgayKham"],
                "SoThuTu": row["SoThuTu"],
                "NgayDangKy": row["NgayDangKy"],
                "TrangThai": row["TrangThai"],
            }
        copied = sorted(
            latest_by_pair.values(),
            key=lambda row: (row["MaDSKham"], row["MaBN"]),
        )

        staged.create(connection)
        if copied:
            connection.execute(staged.insert(), copied)
        staged_rows = connection.execute(
            select(staged).order_by(staged.c.MaDSKham, staged.c.MaBN)
        ).all()
        expected_rows = [
            (
                row["MaDSKham"], row["MaBN"], row["NgayKham"],
                row["SoThuTu"], row["NgayDangKy"], row["TrangThai"],
            )
            for row in copied
        ]
        if staged_rows != expected_rows:
            raise ValueError(
                "Composite-key migration verification failed; original table retained."
            )

        quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
        old_indexes = inspect(connection).get_indexes(table_name)
        if engine.dialect.name in {"mysql", "mariadb"}:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "RENAME TABLE "
                f"{quote(table_name)} TO {quote(backup_name)}, "
                f"{quote(stage_name)} TO {quote(table_name)}"
            )
        else:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"ALTER TABLE {quote(table_name)} RENAME TO {quote(backup_name)}"
            )
            for index in old_indexes:
                if index.get("name"):
                    connection.exec_driver_sql(f"DROP INDEX {quote(index['name'])}")
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"ALTER TABLE {quote(stage_name)} RENAME TO {quote(table_name)}"
            )

        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"CREATE UNIQUE INDEX {quote('uq_ctdsk_ngay_thu_tu')} "
            f"ON {quote(table_name)} ({quote('NgayKham')}, {quote('SoThuTu')})"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"CREATE INDEX {quote('ix_ctdsk_ngay_trang_thai')} "
            f"ON {quote(table_name)} ({quote('NgayKham')}, {quote('TrangThai')})"
        )

    if engine.dialect.name == "sqlite":
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=OFF")
            connection.commit()
            try:
                with connection.begin():
                    saoChepVaThayBang(connection)
            finally:
                connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
                connection.commit()
            violations = connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").all()
            if violations:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Foreign-key violations found after migration; inspect the database."
                )
    else:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            saoChepVaThayBang(connection)
    return True

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from enum import Enum

from sqlalchemy import ForeignKeyConstraint, UniqueConstraint

from app import db


DON_VI_THUOC_CHUAN = (
    ("SAMPLE-VIEN", "ViÃªn"),
    ("SAMPLE-CHAI", "Chai"),
    ("SAMPLE-VI", "Vá»‰"),
)
TEN_DON_VI_THUOC_CHUAN = frozenset(ten for _, ten in DON_VI_THUOC_CHUAN)


class Role(str, Enum):
    PATIENT = "PATIENT"
    NURSE = "NURSE"
    CASHIER = "CASHIER"
    DOCTOR = "DOCTOR"
    ADMIN = "ADMIN"

    @property
    def tenVaiTro(self) -> str:
        return {
            "PATIENT": "Bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n", "NURSE": "Y tÃ¡", "CASHIER": "Thu ngÃ¢n",
            "DOCTOR": "BÃ¡c sÄ©", "ADMIN": "Quáº£n trá»‹",
        }[self.value]


class TaiKhoan(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "TaiKhoan"
    __table_args__ = {"sqlite_autoincrement": True}

    maTK: Mapped[int] = mapped_column("MaTK", db.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    tenDangNhap: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(db.String(80), nullable=False, unique=True)
    matKhau: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(db.String(255), nullable=False)
    role: Mapped[Role] = mapped_column(db.Enum(Role, native_enum=False, create_constraint=True), nullable=False)
    trangThai: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(db.Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)
    nhanVien: Mapped[NhanVien | None] = relationship(back_populates="taiKhoan", uselist=False)
    benhNhan: Mapped[BenhNhan | None] = relationship(back_populates="taiKhoan", uselist=False)

    def get_id(self) -> str:
        return str(self.maTK)

    @property
    def maHoSo(self) -> str:
        profile = self.benhNhan if self.role == Role.PATIENT else self.nhanVien
        return profile.maHienThi if profile else ""

    @property
    def is_active(self) -> bool:
        return self.trangThai

    @property
    def is_authenticated(self) -> bool:
        return True

    @property
    def is_anonymous(self) -> bool:
        return False

    @property
    def hoTen(self) -> str:
        profile = self.benhNhan if self.role == Role.PATIENT else self.nhanVien
        return profile.hoTen if profile else self.tenDangNhap

    def datMatKhau(self, password: str) -> None:
        self.matKhau = generate_password_hash(password)

    def dangNhap(self, tenDangNhap: str, matKhau: str) -> bool:
        return (
            self.trangThai and self.tenDangNhap == tenDangNhap.strip().lower()
            and check_password_hash(self.matKhau, matKhau)
        )

    def dangXuat(self) -> bool:
        from flask_login import current_user, logout_user

        if not current_user.is_authenticated or current_user.get_id() != self.get_id():
            return False
        logout_user()
        return True

    def doiMatKhau(self, tenDangNhap: str, matKhau: str) -> bool:
        # Authorization and password policy are enforced by the calling DAO.
        if self.tenDangNhap != tenDangNhap.strip().lower():
            raise ValueError("TÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p khÃ´ng khá»›p tÃ i khoáº£n.")
        self.datMatKhau(matKhau)
        return True


class NhanVien(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "NhanVien"
    __table_args__ = {"sqlite_autoincrement": True}

    maNV: Mapped[int] = mapped_column("MaNV", db.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    hoTen: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(db.String(100), nullable=False)
    soDienThoai: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(db.String(20), nullable=False)
    maTK: Mapped[int] = mapped_column("MaTK", db.ForeignKey("TaiKhoan.MaTK"), unique=True, nullable=False)
    taiKhoan: Mapped[TaiKhoan] = relationship(back_populates="nhanVien")
    bacSi: Mapped[BacSi | None] = relationship(back_populates="nhanVien", uselist=False)
    danhSachKhams: Mapped[list[DanhSachKham]] = relationship(back_populates="nhanVien")

    @property
    def maHienThi(self) -> str:
        prefix = {
            Role.NURSE: "YT", Role.CASHIER: "TN", Role.DOCTOR: "BS", Role.ADMIN: "QT",
        }.get(self.taiKhoan.role, "NV")
        return f"{prefix}-{self.maNV:04d}"

    __tablename__ = "ChiTietDonThuoc"

    maThuoc: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        "MaThuoc", db.ForeignKey("Thuoc.MaThuoc"), primary_key=True,
    )
    maPhieuKham: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        "MaPhieuKham", db.ForeignKey("PhieuKham.MaPhieuKham"), primary_key=True,
    )
    soLuong: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(db.Integer, nullable=False)
    cachDung: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(db.String(255), nullable=False)
    thuoc: Mapped[Thuoc] = relationship(back_populates="chiTietDonThuocs")
    phieuKham: Mapped[PhieuKham] = relationship(back_populates="chiTietDonThuocs")


class HoaDon(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "HoaDon"
    __table_args__ = {"sqlite_autoincrement": True}

    maHoaDon: Mapped[int] = mapped_column("MaHoaDon", db.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    ngayLap: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column("NgayLap", db.DateTime, nullable=False)
    tienKham: Mapped[float] = mapped_column("TienKham", db.Double, nullable=False, default=0)
    tienThuoc: Mapped[float] = mapped_column("TienThuoc", db.Double, nullable=False, default=0)
    tongTien: Mapped[float] = mapped_column("TongTien", db.Double, nullable=False, default=0)
    phuongThuc: Mapped[str] = mapped_column("PhuongThuc", db.String(20), nullable=False, default="TIEN_MAT")
    trangThai: Mapped[str] = mapped_column("TrangThai", db.String(20), nullable=False, default="CHUA_THANH_TOAN")
    maPhieuKham: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        "MaPhieuKham", db.ForeignKey("PhieuKham.MaPhieuKham"), nullable=False, unique=True,
    )
    maNV: Mapped[int] = mapped_column("MaNV", db.ForeignKey("NhanVien.MaNV"), nullable=False)
    phieuKham: Mapped[PhieuKham] = relationship()
    nhanVien: Mapped[NhanVien] = relationship()


class QuyDinh(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "QuyDinh"
    __table_args__ = (
        db.CheckConstraint("giaTri > 0", name="ck_rule_positive"),
    )

    maQD: Mapped[str] = mapped_column("MaQD", db.String(30), primary_key=True)
    tenQuyDinh: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(db.String(100), nullable=False)
    giaTri: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(db.Numeric(15, 2), nullable=False)

    def capNhatTienKham(self, giaTriMoi: Decimal) -> None:
        if self.maQD != "TIEN_KHAM":
            raise ValueError("ÄÃ¢y khÃ´ng pháº£i quy Ä‘á»‹nh tiá»n khÃ¡m.")
        if not giaTriMoi.is_finite() or not Decimal("0") < giaTriMoi <= Decimal("9999999999999.99"):
            raise ValueError("Tiá»n khÃ¡m pháº£i lá»›n hÆ¡n 0 vÃ  náº±m trong giá»›i háº¡n lÆ°u trá»¯.")
        if giaTriMoi != giaTriMoi.quantize(Decimal("0.01")):
            raise ValueError("Tiá»n khÃ¡m chá»‰ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cÃ³ tá»‘i Ä‘a 2 chá»¯ sá»‘ tháº­p phÃ¢n.")
        self.giaTri = giaTriMoi

    def capNhatQuyDinhKham(self, soBenhNhan: int) -> None:
        if self.maQD != "SO_BENH_NHAN_TOI_DA":
            raise ValueError("ÄÃ¢y khÃ´ng pháº£i quy Ä‘á»‹nh sá»‘ bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n tá»‘i Ä‘a.")
        if isinstance(soBenhNhan, bool) or not 1 <= soBenhNhan <= 100000:
            raise ValueError("Sá»‘ bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n tá»‘i Ä‘a pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘ nguyÃªn tá»« 1 Ä‘áº¿n 100000.")
        self.giaTri = Decimal(soBenhNhan)

    def laySoBenhNhanToiDa(self) -> int:
        if (self.maQD != "SO_BENH_NHAN_TOI_DA"
                or self.giaTri != self.giaTri.to_integral_value() or not 1 <= self.giaTri <= 100000):
            raise ValueError("Quy Ä‘á»‹nh sá»‘ bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n tá»‘i Ä‘a khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.")
        return int(self.giaTri)

import re
import secrets
from datetime import date
from functools import wraps
import os
import requests

from flask import abort, request, session
from flask_login import current_user

from app.models import Role

ESMS_URL = "https://rest.esms.vn/MainService.svc/json/SendMultipleMessage_V4_post_json/"

def taoMaCsrf() -> str:
    if "csrf_token" not in session:
        session["csrf_token"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    return session["csrf_token"]


def baoVeCsrf() -> None:
    if request.method in {"POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE"}:
        supplied = request.form.get("csrf_token") or request.headers.get("X-CSRF-Token", "")
        expected = session.get("csrf_token", "")
        if not supplied or not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(
            supplied.encode("utf-8"), expected.encode("utf-8")
        ):
            abort(400, description="PhiÃªn biá»ƒu máº«u khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡. HÃ£y táº£i láº¡i trang vÃ  thá»­ láº¡i.")


def yeuCauVaiTro(*roles: Role):
    def trangTriTheoVaiTro(function):
        @wraps(function)
        def kiemTraVaiTro(*args, **kwargs):
            if not current_user.is_authenticated:
                from app import login
                return login.unauthorized()
            if current_user.role not in roles:
                abort(403)
            return function(*args, **kwargs)
        return kiemTraVaiTro
    return trangTriTheoVaiTro


def kiemTraMatKhau(password: str) -> None:
    if not 10 <= len(password) <= 128:
        raise ValueError("Máº­t kháº©u pháº£i cÃ³ tá»« 10 Ä‘áº¿n 128 kÃ½ tá»±.")


def layDuLieuTaiKhoan(form, *, registration: bool = False) -> dict:
    username = form.get("tenDangNhap", "").strip().lower()
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z0-9_.-]{3,80}", username):
        raise ValueError("TÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p gá»“m 3â€“80 chá»¯ khÃ´ng dáº¥u, sá»‘ hoáº·c kÃ½ tá»± _ . -")
    try:
        role = Role.PATIENT if registration else Role(form.get("role", ""))
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError("Vai trÃ² khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.") from None
    name = form.get("hoTen", "").strip()
    phone = form.get("soDienThoai", "").strip()
    if not name or len(name) > 100:
        raise ValueError("Há» tÃªn báº¯t buá»™c vÃ  tá»‘i Ä‘a 100 kÃ½ tá»±.")
    if (role == Role.PATIENT and not phone) or (
        phone and not re.fullmatch(r"\+?[0-9]{9,15}", phone)
    ):
        raise ValueError("Sá»‘ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i pháº£i cÃ³ 9â€“15 chá»¯ sá»‘, cÃ³ thá»ƒ báº¯t Ä‘áº§u báº±ng +.")
    data = {
        "tenDangNhap": username, "role": role, "hoTen": name,
        "soDienThoai": phone, "password": form.get("matKhau", ""),
        "trangThai": True if registration else form.get("trangThai") == "on",
    }
    if role == Role.PATIENT:
        try:
            year = int(form.get("namSinh", ""))
        except ValueError:
            raise ValueError("NÄƒm sinh khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.") from None
        gender = form.get("gioiTinh", "")
        address = form.get("diaChi", "").strip()
        if not 1900 <= year <= date.today().year:
            raise ValueError("NÄƒm sinh pháº£i tá»« 1900 Ä‘áº¿n nÄƒm hiá»‡n táº¡i.")
        if gender not in {"Nam", "Ná»¯", "KhÃ¡c"} or not address or len(address) > 255:
            raise ValueError("Vui lÃ²ng chá»n giá»›i tÃ­nh vÃ  nháº­p Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ tá»‘i Ä‘a 255 kÃ½ tá»±.")
        data.update(namSinh=year, gioiTinh=gender, diaChi=address)
    if role == Role.DOCTOR:
        specialty = form.get("chuyenKhoa", "").strip()
        if not specialty or len(specialty) > 100:
            raise ValueError("ChuyÃªn khoa báº¯t buá»™c vÃ  tá»‘i Ä‘a 100 kÃ½ tá»±.")
        data["chuyenKhoa"] = specialty
    return data

def guiTinNhanSms(phone, content):
    mode = os.getenv("SMS_MODE", "mock")
    if mode == "mock":
        print(f"[MOCK SMS] {phone}: {content}")
        return True, {"mode": "mock"}

    payload = {
        "ApiKey": os.getenv("ESMS_API_KEY"),
        "SecretKey": os.getenv("ESMS_SECRET_KEY"),
        "Phone": phone,
        "Content": content,
        "Brandname": "Baotrixemay",
        "SmsType": "2",
        "IsUnicode": "0",
        "Sandbox": "1" if mode == "sandbox" else "0",
    }
    try:
        res = requests.post(ESMS_URL, json=payload, timeout=10)
        data = res.json()
        print("ESMS:", data, "| phone:", phone, "| mode:", mode)
        return data.get("CodeResult") == "100", data
    except (requests.RequestException, ValueError) as e:
        return False, {"error": str(e)}

import os
import secrets
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import ssl
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import certifi
import click
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from flask import Flask, flash, redirect, session, url_for
from flask_sqlalchemy import SQLAlchemy
from flask_login import LoginManager
from sqlalchemy import URL, make_url, text
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError


load_dotenv(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / ".env")

app = Flask(__name__)
secret_path = Path(app.instance_path) / "session-secret"
if not os.environ.get("SECRET_KEY"):
    secret_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        with secret_path.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as secret_file:
            secret_file.write(secrets.token_hex(32))
    except FileExistsError:
        pass
app.config["SECRET_KEY"] = os.environ.get("SECRET_KEY") or secret_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
app.config.update(
    SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY=True,
    SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE="Lax",
    SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=os.environ.get("COOKIE_SECURE") == "1",
    MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH=64 * 1024,
)
if os.environ.get("DATABASE_URL"):
    database_url = make_url(os.environ["DATABASE_URL"])
elif os.environ.get("MYSQL_HOST"):
    if not os.environ.get("MYSQL_PASSWORD"):
        raise RuntimeError("Set MYSQL_PASSWORD in .env before connecting to TiDB.")
    database_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username=os.environ["MYSQL_USER"],
        password=os.environ["MYSQL_PASSWORD"],
        host=os.environ["MYSQL_HOST"],
        port=int(os.environ.get("MYSQL_PORT", "4000")),
        database=os.environ["MYSQL_DATABASE"],
        query={"charset": "utf8mb4"},
    )
else:
    database_url = make_url("sqlite:///medical.db")

if database_url.drivername == "mysql":
    database_url = database_url.set(drivername="mysql+pymysql")

app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = database_url
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False

if database_url.drivername == "mysql+pymysql":
    app.config["SQLALCHEMY_ENGINE_OPTIONS"] = {
        "pool_pre_ping": True,
        "connect_args": {
            "ssl": ssl.create_default_context(cafile=certifi.where()),
            "connect_timeout": 10,
        },
    }

db = SQLAlchemy(app)
login = LoginManager(app)


@login.unauthorized_handler
def xuLyChuaDangNhap():
    flash("Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p Ä‘á»ƒ tiáº¿p tá»¥c.", "info")
    return redirect(url_for("login_view"))


@login.user_loader
def taiNguoiDung(account_id: str):
    from app.models import TaiKhoan
    if not account_id.isascii() or not account_id.isdecimal() or len(account_id) > 10:
        return None
    account = db.session.get(TaiKhoan, int(account_id))
    if not account or not account.trangThai:
        return None
    fingerprint = hashlib.sha256(account.matKhau.encode()).hexdigest()
    return account if secrets.compare_digest(session.get("auth_hash", ""), fingerprint) else None


@app.cli.command("check-db")
def kiemTraCoSoDuLieu() -> None:
    """Verify connectivity without creating tables or modifying data."""
    with db.engine.connect() as connection:
        connection.execute(text("SELECT 1")).scalar_one()
    click.echo("Database connection successful.")


@app.cli.command("init-db")
def khoiTaoCoSoDuLieu() -> None:
    """Create tables missing from the current models; never alter existing schema."""
    from app import models
    from sqlalchemy import inspect
    from app.models import ChiTietDanhSachKham

    inspector = inspect(db.engine)
    existing_tables = set(inspector.get_table_names())
    if "DanhSachKham" in existing_tables and "ChiTietDanhSachKham" not in existing_tables:
        day_columns = {
            column["name"] for column in inspector.get_columns("DanhSachKham")
        }
        if "MaBN" in day_columns:
            tables = [
                table for table in db.metadata.sorted_tables
                if table is not ChiTietDanhSachKham.__table__
            ]
            db.create_all(tables=tables)
            click.echo(
                "Existing registration schema retained; run "
                "upgrade-nurse-many-to-many-schema while writers are stopped."
            )
            return
    db.create_all()
    click.echo("Tables missing from the current models are ready.")


@app.cli.command("create-patient-registration-table")
def taoBangDangKyKham() -> None:
    """Create the daily exam list and patient-association tables if missing."""
    from sqlalchemy import inspect
    from app.models import ChiTietDanhSachKham, DanhSachKham

    existing_tables = set(inspect(db.engine).get_table_names())
    if not {"BenhNhan", "NhanVien"} <= existing_tables:
        raise click.ClickException(
            "BenhNhan or NhanVien is missing; run init-db first."
        )
    if "DanhSachKham" in existing_tables and "ChiTietDanhSachKham" not in existing_tables:
        day_columns = {
            column["name"] for column in inspect(db.engine).get_columns("DanhSachKham")
        }
        if "MaBN" in day_columns:
            raise click.ClickException(
                "DanhSachKham uses the older registration schema; run "
                "upgrade-nurse-many-to-many-schema with writers stopped."
            )
    try:
        db.metadata.create_all(
            bind=db.engine,
            tables=[DanhSachKham.__table__, ChiTietDanhSachKham.__table__],
        )
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        app.logger.exception("Patient registration table creation failed.")
        raise click.ClickException(
            "Could not create exam-list tables; inspect the database before retrying."
        ) from None
    click.echo("DanhSachKham and ChiTietDanhSachKham are ready.")


@app.cli.command("upgrade-clinical-schema")
def nangCapCauTrucPhieuKham() -> None:
    """Add clinical status and diagnosis columns without changing existing exam rows."""
    from sqlalchemy import inspect

    inspector = inspect(db.engine)
    if "PhieuKham" not in inspector.get_table_names():
        raise click.ClickException("PhieuKham is missing; run init-db first.")
    quote = db.engine.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
    columns = {column["name"] for column in inspector.get_columns("PhieuKham")}
    statements = []
    if "trangThai" not in columns:
        statements.append(
            f"ALTER TABLE {quote('PhieuKham')} ADD COLUMN {quote('trangThai')} "
            "VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DA_LAP'"
        )
    if "DuDoanLoaiBenh" not in columns:
        statements.append(
            f"ALTER TABLE {quote('PhieuKham')} ADD COLUMN {quote('DuDoanLoaiBenh')} "
            "TEXT NULL"
        )
    if not statements:
        click.echo("PhieuKham.trangThai already exists; DuDoanLoaiBenh already exists.")
        return
    try:
        with db.engine.begin() as connection:
            for statement in statements:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(statement)
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        app.logger.exception("Clinical schema upgrade failed.")
        raise click.ClickException(
            "Could not add PhieuKham clinical columns; inspect the database before retrying."
        ) from None
    click.echo("PhieuKham clinical columns added; existing exams keep DA_LAP.")


@app.cli.command("upgrade-nurse-queue-schema")
def nangCapCauTrucHangCho() -> None:
    """Prepare patient profiles and daily registration rows for the nurse queue."""
    from app.migration import nangCapCauTrucHangCho as upgrade_schema

    try:
        changed = upgrade_schema(db.engine)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise click.ClickException(str(error)) from None
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        app.logger.exception("Nurse queue schema upgrade failed.")
        raise click.ClickException(
            "Could not upgrade the nurse queue schema; inspect the database before retrying."
        ) from None
    if changed:
        click.echo(
            "Nurse queue schema is ready; existing registrations were preserved and dated."
        )
    else:
        click.echo("Nurse queue schema already exists; no changes made.")

def lenhChuyenDangKyChoCu(maintenance: bool) -> None:
    """Move legacy waiting registrations to the next available exam day."""

        raise click.ClickException("Could not move waiting registrations; see application log.") from None
    click.echo(f"Moved {moved} waiting registration(s).")

@app.cli.command("seed-doctor-demo")
def taoDuLieuMauBacSi() -> None:
    """Create clearly labeled sample records for the doctor workflow."""
    from sqlalchemy import inspect
    from app.models import (
        BacSi, BenhNhan, DON_VI_THUOC_CHUAN, DonViThuoc, NhanVien, PhieuKham,
        Role, TaiKhoan, Thuoc,
    )

    required = {"TaiKhoan", "NhanVien", "BacSi", "BenhNhan",
                "DonViThuoc", "Thuoc", "PhieuKham", "ChiTietDonThuoc"}
    missing = required - set(inspect(db.engine).get_table_names())
    if missing:
        raise click.ClickException(
            "Missing tables: " + ", ".join(sorted(missing)) + ". Run init-db first."
        )

    username = "demo_quynh_test"
    account = db.session.scalar(
        db.select(TaiKhoan).where(TaiKhoan.tenDangNhap == username)
    )
    if account is None:
        account = TaiKhoan(
            tenDangNhap=username, role=Role.PATIENT, trangThai=False,
        )
        account.datMatKhau(secrets.token_urlsafe(32))
        account.benhNhan = BenhNhan(
            hoTen="Nguyá»…n KhÃ¡nh NhÆ° Quá»³nh",
            gioiTinh="KhÃ¡c",
            namSinh=2000,
            diaChi="Há»“ sÆ¡ kiá»ƒm thá»­ - khÃ´ng pháº£i Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ tháº­t",
            soDienThoai="0000000000",
        )
        db.session.add(account)
    elif (
        account.role != Role.PATIENT or account.benhNhan is None
        or account.benhNhan.hoTen != "Nguyá»…n KhÃ¡nh NhÆ° Quá»³nh"
    ):
        raise click.ClickException(
            "demo_quynh_test is not the expected demo patient; refusing to modify it."
        )

    ma_don_vi_vien, ten_don_vi_vien = DON_VI_THUOC_CHUAN[0]
    don_vi_vien = db.session.scalar(
        db.select(DonViThuoc).where(DonViThuoc.tenDonVi == ten_don_vi_vien)
    )
    if don_vi_vien is None:
        don_vi_vien = db.session.get(DonViThuoc, ma_don_vi_vien)
        if don_vi_vien is not None:
            raise click.ClickException(
                f"Unit code {ma_don_vi_vien} already belongs to other data."
            )
        don_vi_vien = DonViThuoc(maDonVi=ma_don_vi_vien, tenDonVi=ten_don_vi_vien)
        db.session.add(don_vi_vien)

    demo_medicines = (
        ("DEMO-T001", "DEMO - ViÃªn nÃ©n thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 100),
        ("DEMO-T002", "DEMO - Dung dá»‹ch thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 100),
        ("DEMO-T003", "DEMO - GÃ³i bá»™t thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 100),
        ("DEMO-T004", "DEMO - ViÃªn nang thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 100),
        ("DEMO-T005", "DEMO - Lá» máº«u thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 100),
        ("DEMO-T006", "DEMO - TuÃ½p máº«u thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 100),
        ("DEMO-T007", "DEMO - á»ng máº«u thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 100),
        ("DEMO-T008", "DEMO - Há»™p máº«u thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 100),
    )
    for code, name, stock in demo_medicines:
        medicine = db.session.get(Thuoc, code)
        if medicine is None:
            db.session.add(Thuoc(
                maThuoc=code, tenThuoc=name, donGia=0,
                soLuongTon=stock, donVi=don_vi_vien,
            ))
        elif not medicine.tenThuoc.startswith("DEMO -"):
            raise click.ClickException(f"Medicine code {code} already belongs to non-demo data.")

    additional_patients = (
        ("demo_patient_01", "DEMO - Nguyá»…n Minh An"),
        ("demo_patient_02", "DEMO - Tráº§n Gia Báº£o"),
        ("demo_patient_03", "DEMO - LÃª HoÃ ng Mai"),
        ("demo_patient_04", "DEMO - Pháº¡m Äá»©c Minh"),
        ("demo_patient_05", "DEMO - VÃµ Thanh HÃ "),
        ("demo_patient_06", "DEMO - Äáº·ng Quá»‘c Huy"),
        ("demo_patient_07", "DEMO - BÃ¹i Ngá»c Lan"),
        ("demo_patient_08", "DEMO - Äá»— Tuáº¥n Kiá»‡t"),
        ("demo_patient_09", "DEMO - Há»“ KhÃ¡nh Linh"),
        ("demo_patient_10", "DEMO - Phan Nháº­t Nam"),
    )
    for index, (patient_username, patient_name) in enumerate(additional_patients, 1):
        demo_account = db.session.scalar(
            db.select(TaiKhoan).where(TaiKhoan.tenDangNhap == patient_username)
        )
        if demo_account is None:
            demo_account = TaiKhoan(
                tenDangNhap=patient_username, role=Role.PATIENT, trangThai=False,
            )
            demo_account.datMatKhau(secrets.token_urlsafe(32))
            demo_account.benhNhan = BenhNhan(
                hoTen=patient_name,
                gioiTinh="KhÃ¡c",
                namSinh=1990 + index,
                diaChi="Há»“ sÆ¡ kiá»ƒm thá»­ - khÃ´ng pháº£i Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ tháº­t",
                soDienThoai="0000000000",
            )
            db.session.add(demo_account)
        elif (
            demo_account.role != Role.PATIENT or demo_account.benhNhan is None
            or demo_account.benhNhan.hoTen != patient_name
        ):
            raise click.ClickException(
                f"{patient_username} is not the expected demo patient; refusing to modify it."
            )

    clinic_timezone = timezone(timedelta(hours=7))
    today = datetime.now(clinic_timezone).date()
    patient = account.benhNhan
    if patient is None:
        raise click.ClickException("Demo patient profile is missing.")
    marker = "Dá»® LIá»†U KIá»‚M THá»¬ - khÃ´ng pháº£i thÃ´ng tin y táº¿ tháº­t."
    existing_exam = db.session.scalar(
        db.select(PhieuKham).where(
            PhieuKham.maBN == patient.maBN,
            PhieuKham.trieuChung == marker,
        )
    )
    doctor = db.session.scalar(
        db.select(BacSi)
        .join(NhanVien)
        .join(TaiKhoan)
        .where(TaiKhoan.role == Role.DOCTOR, TaiKhoan.trangThai.is_(True))
        .order_by(BacSi.maNV)
    )
    existing_demo_exam_id = db.session.get(PhieuKham, "DEMO-PK-QUYNH")
    if (
        existing_demo_exam_id is not None
        and (existing_demo_exam_id.maBN != patient.maBN
             or existing_demo_exam_id.trieuChung != marker)
    ):
        raise click.ClickException(
            "Demo exam ID already belongs to other data; refusing to modify it."
        )
    if existing_exam is None and existing_demo_exam_id is None and doctor is not None:
        db.session.add(PhieuKham(
            maPhieuKham="DEMO-PK-QUYNH",
            ngayKham=today - timedelta(days=1),
            trieuChung=marker,
            duDoanLoaiBenh="Dá»¯ liá»‡u kiá»ƒm thá»­ - khÃ´ng pháº£i cháº©n Ä‘oÃ¡n tháº­t.",
            benhNhan=patient,
            bacSi=doctor,
        ))

    try:
        db.session.commit()
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        db.session.rollback()
        app.logger.exception("Doctor demo seed failed.")
        raise click.ClickException("Could not save demo records; see application log.") from None

    click.echo(f"Demo patient ready: {patient.maHienThi}")
    click.echo("Eleven demo patient profiles and eight demo medicines are ready.")
    click.echo("All demo patient accounts are disabled; inventory was not changed.")
    click.echo("Demo history record is ready." if doctor else
               "No active doctor profile exists; history record was not created.")


@app.cli.command("create-admin")
@click.option("--username", prompt=True)
@click.option("--password", prompt=True, hide_input=True, confirmation_prompt=True)
@click.option("--name", prompt=True)
@click.option("--phone", default="", help="Optional employee phone number.")
def taoQuanTriVien(username: str, password: str, name: str, phone: str) -> None:
    from app.dao import taoTaiKhoan
    from app.utils import layDuLieuTaiKhoan
    try:
        data = layDuLieuTaiKhoan({
            "tenDangNhap": username, "matKhau": password, "hoTen": name,
            "soDienThoai": phone, "role": "ADMIN", "trangThai": "on",
        })
        taoTaiKhoan(data)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise click.ClickException(str(error)) from error
    click.echo("Administrator created.")


@app.cli.command("seed-admin-demo")
def taoDuLieuMauQuanTri() -> None:
    """Initialize approved rules and three labeled medicine samples, without overwriting data."""
    from decimal import Decimal
    from app.models import DON_VI_THUOC_CHUAN, DonViThuoc, QuyDinh, Thuoc

    try:
        rules = (
            ("TIEN_KHAM", "Tiá»n khÃ¡m", Decimal("100000")),
            ("SO_BENH_NHAN_TOI_DA", "Sá»‘ bá»‡nh nhÃ¢n tá»‘i Ä‘a trong ngÃ y", Decimal("40")),
        )
        for code, name, value in rules:
            if db.session.get(QuyDinh, code) is None:
                rule = QuyDinh()
                rule.maQD, rule.tenQuyDinh, rule.giaTri = code, name, value
                db.session.add(rule)
        ma_don_vi_theo_ten = {}
        for ma_mac_dinh, ten in DON_VI_THUOC_CHUAN:
            unit = db.session.scalar(
                db.select(DonViThuoc).where(DonViThuoc.tenDonVi == ten)
            )
            if unit is None:
                unit = db.session.get(DonViThuoc, ma_mac_dinh)
                if unit is not None:
                    raise ValueError(f"MÃ£ {ma_mac_dinh} Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c sá»­ dá»¥ng cho Ä‘Æ¡n vá»‹ khÃ¡c.")
                unit = DonViThuoc(maDonVi=ma_mac_dinh, tenDonVi=ten)
                db.session.add(unit)
            ma_don_vi_theo_ten[ten] = unit.maDonVi
        for code, name, price, unit in (
            ("SAMPLE-T001", "MáºªU - ViÃªn thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 5000, "ViÃªn"),
            ("SAMPLE-T002", "MáºªU - Dung dá»‹ch thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 25000, "Chai"),
            ("SAMPLE-T003", "MáºªU - Thuá»‘c thá»­ nghiá»‡m (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u trá»‹)", 45000, "ViÃªn"),
        ):
            medicine = db.session.get(Thuoc, code)
            if medicine is None:
                medicine = Thuoc()
                medicine.maThuoc, medicine.tenThuoc, medicine.donGia = code, name, price
                medicine.soLuongTon, medicine.maDonVi = 10, ma_don_vi_theo_ten[unit]
                db.session.add(medicine)
            elif medicine.tenThuoc != name:
                raise ValueError(f"MÃ£ {code} Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c sá»­ dá»¥ng cho thuá»‘c khÃ¡c.")
        db.session.commit()
    except ValueError as error:
        db.session.rollback()
        raise click.ClickException(str(error)) from error
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        db.session.rollback()
        app.logger.exception("Admin sample initialization failed.")
        raise click.ClickException("KhÃ´ng khá»Ÿi táº¡o Ä‘Æ°á»£c dá»¯ liá»‡u máº«u; xem log á»©ng dá»¥ng.") from None
    click.echo("Rules and three sample medicines ready; existing data was not overwritten.")


@app.cli.command("migrate-account-ids")
@click.option("--maintenance", is_flag=True, help="Confirm all application writers are stopped.")
def lenhChuyenMaTaiKhoan(maintenance: bool) -> None:
    """Preserve old UUID tables and migrate account/profile keys to integers."""
    from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError
    from app.migration import chuyenMaTaiKhoan
    if not maintenance:
        raise click.ClickException("Stop all application writers and pass --maintenance.")
    try:
        changed = chuyenMaTaiKhoan(db.engine)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise click.ClickException(str(error)) from error
    except SQLAlchemyError as error:
        app.logger.error("Identifier migration failed: %s", type(error).__name__)
        raise click.ClickException(
            "Migration failed. Original/backup tables were not deleted; inspect before retrying."
        ) from error
    click.echo("Integer IDs ready; original tables retained as *_uuid_backup." if changed
               else "Identifiers are already integers; no changes made.")
